Conclusion: 在高风险决策场景中，应从根本上重新审视基于方差的降维方法，PCA可能产生危险的误导性结果，而ExPCA和TP-PCA提供了更安全的替代方案。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) preserves variance, not the information needed to detect rare catastrophic events. This paper proves the existence of a {\it Risk Shadow}: PCA can retain over 99.9999 percent of total variance while completely erasing all signal about rare, high-impact failures. When this happens, even the best possible classifier operating on the PCA representation reduces to a constant predictor. The root cause is a fundamental mismatch between variance maximization and tail risk awareness. To break the shadow, we introduce Expectile PCA (ExPCA) and Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA), two methods that reweight the data covariance toward high-impact events. We prove theoretically that ExPCA strictly outperforms PCA in retaining rare-event information, and we validate our claims on synthetic data and a real-world credit card fraud detection benchmark. Our results call for a fundamental rethinking of variance-based dimensionality reduction in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [85] [CARE: Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14581)
*Guanyu Liu,Weiyi Kong,Zeyu Wang,Boer Zhang,Baiqing Li,Peiyu Zhang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE是一个可审计的控制器，通过保留非LLM优化器作为默认路径，仅在公开证据支持时才授权LLM修改挑战者排序策略，从而在科学实验中安全利用LLM创造力，在Minerva/Olympus和ChemLex基准上分别将最终最佳性能从80.0提升至88.5、从83.9提升至92.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接让LLM控制昂贵且不可逆的科学实验会导致不安全的探索和不稳定的性能，但完全舍弃LLM的创造力又会损失显著的优化潜力，因此需要一种既能利用LLM优势又能确保安全性和可审计性的方法。

Method: CARE框架包含一个非LLM的现有优化器作为默认执行路径，LLM用于修改挑战者排序策略；在每次实验结果揭晓前，基于公开证据的干预门控机制比较挑战者与现有方案，仅当选择前可获取的证据支持变更时才授权选择挑战者，所有决策均记录在审计日志中。

Result: 在Minerva/Olympus基准上最终最佳性能从80.0提升至88.5，在ChemLex基准上从83.9提升至92.1，优于所有其他评估方法。

Conclusion: LLM的自我进化在可审计控制器下扩展提议空间比直接选择实验更可靠。

Abstract: Granting LLMs direct control over costly, irreversible scientific experiments leads to unsafe exploration and unstable performance, but discarding LLM creativity entirely sacrifices significant optimization potential. We introduce CARE (Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation), an auditable controller for high-throughput experimentation (HTE) optimization that keeps a non-LLM incumbent optimizer as the default action path while using LLMs to revise challenger ranking policies. Before each outcome is revealed, a public-evidence intervention gate compares the challenger with the incumbent. It authorizes the challenger's selection only when the evidence available before selection supports the change, with the decision recorded in the audit log. CARE outperforms all other evaluated methods on Minerva/Olympus and ChemLex benchmarks, with final-best improving from 80.0 to 88.5 on Minerva/Olympus and from 83.9 to 92.1 on ChemLex, relative to the public incumbent. Our experiments indicate that LLM self-evolution is more reliable when it expands the proposal space under an auditable controller, rather than directly choosing experiments.

</details>


### [86] [Zero-shot generalization of transformer neural operators to larger domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14597)
*Armand de Villeroché,Sibo Cheng,Vincent Le Guen,Marc Bocquet,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Patrick Armand,Alban Farchi,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可分解的注意力局部性偏置方法，结合旋转位置编码，使基于Transformer的神经算子能够在零样本条件下泛化到训练时未见的大规模空间域，显著提升了在PDE和3D工业大气流动应用上的域扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子隐式假设固定域大小，在推理时无法泛化到更大空间域，亟需一种支持零样本域扩展的方法。

Method: 在注意力logits计算中引入可分解的局部性偏置（decomposable bias），使其保持查询-键内积形式并兼容优化注意力内核；结合旋转位置编码（RoPE）实现可控空间支持的表达性嵌入，且不改变Transformer架构。

Result: 在两个PDE基准和一个3D工业大气流动应用中，该方法在零样本泛化到更大域时显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 空间局部性和平移等变性是域扩展的关键，所提可分解局部性偏置有效实现了零样本大域泛化。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have shown remarkable performance for approximating solution operators of partial differential equations on complex geometries. However, existing approaches implicitly assume a fixed domain size, which limits their ability to generalize at inference. In this work, we investigate domain extension, namely zero-shot inference on spatial domains that are significantly larger than those encountered during training. We argue that this setting fundamentally requires spatial locality and translation equivariance. We propose to implement this locality via a decomposable bias in the attention logits computation, enabling finely controllable locality while remaining fully decomposable into query-key inner products and directly compatible with optimized attention kernels. Combined with rotary positional embeddings, it enables expressive embeddings with controllable spatial support without altering the transformer architecture. We empirically show that our approach substantially improves zero-shot generalization to larger domains across two PDE benchmarks and a 3D industrial atmospheric flow application. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/domain-extension.

</details>


### [87] [Realizing Native INT8 Compute for Diffusion Transformers on Consumer GPUs: A Fused INT8 GEMM Kernel for Ideogram 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14598)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散Transformer的INT8后训练量化在消费级Ampere GPU上反而比FP8和NF4更慢的问题，本文发现其根源在于"INT8"实现实际是反量化回bf16再执行bf16矩阵乘法，并未利用GPU的INT8张量核心。作者通过一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM内核（int8×int8→int32，含per-token×per-channel反量化和bias融合，按GEMM形状自动调优），替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中的反量化到bf16路径，实现了每GEMM 2.8-4.2倍加速，端到端约1.1倍加速（9-10%），在单卡RTX 3090上1024px生成时间156.5秒，超越NF4（164.5秒）和FP8（172.9秒），且无质量损失。但该优化仅适用于消费级Ampere，在A100和B200上不如原生bf16/FP8路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer的INT8 W8A8后训练量化在消费级Ampere GPU上实际比FP8和NF4更慢，原因是其实现仅做了"假INT8"——先把权重和激活量化到INT8再立即反量化回bf16执行bf16矩阵乘法，从未真正利用GPU的INT8张量核心，硬件计算优势完全未发挥。

Method: 设计并实现了一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM内核：(1) 在Ampere张量核心上执行int8×int8→int32矩阵乘法；(2) 在epilogue中融合per-token×per-channel反量化和bias；(3) 按GEMM形状自动调优。将该内核直接替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中的原反量化到bf16路径。

Result: 内核级int8×int8→int32累加与torch._int_mm位精确，反量化输出余弦相似度1.0且无NaN，每GEMM比bf16快2.8-4.2倍。端到端：768px加速约1.1倍（9-10%）；1024px单卡RTX 3090生成时间156.5s，优于NF4（164.5s）和FP8（172.9s），PickScore/CLIPScore无质量损失。主目标（击败FP8，约9.5%）达成；NF4差距（约4.9%，单次n=4）在运行方差内。

Conclusion: INT8从最慢变体变为最快，使1024px生成在单消费级GPU上可行。但该优化具有特定硬件局限性：仅适用于消费级Ampere GPU，在A100和B200上该内核反而不如这些显卡的原生bf16/FP8路径。

Abstract: Post-training INT8 (W8A8) quantization of diffusion transformers is widely deployed as a speed optimization, yet on consumer Ampere GPUs it is frequently slower than the FP8 and NF4 alternatives it is meant to beat. We trace this to a software artifact: the production "INT8" forward quantizes weights and activations only to immediately dequantize them back to bf16 and run a bf16 matrix multiply, never engaging the GPU's INT8 tensor cores, so the hardware's compute advantage is left entirely unrealized. We close this gap with a single fused Triton INT8 GEMM (int8xint8->int32 on Ampere tensor cores, with per-token x per-channel dequantization and bias folded into the epilogue, autotuned per GEMM shape) dropped into the Ideogram 4.0 diffusion transformer's linear layers in place of the dequantize-to-bf16 path. In the kernel, the int8xint8->int32 accumulation is bit-exact against torch._int_mm and the dequantized output matches the reference at cosine similarity 1.0 with no NaNs, running 2.8-4.2x faster than bf16 per GEMM. End to end it delivers a ~1.1x (~9-10%) speedup at 768px, and at 1024px it generates an image in 156.5 s on a single RTX 3090, faster than the single-card NF4 (164.5 s) and FP8 (172.9 s) baselines, at no measurable quality cost on these point estimates (PickScore/CLIPScore). INT8 thus goes from the slowest variant to the fastest, and 1024px becomes single-GPU feasible. The primary speed criterion (beat FP8, by ~9.5%) is comfortably met; the NF4 margin (~4.9%, single-run n=4) is within run-to-run variance we did not quantify and is best read as consistent with meeting the stretch target. We close with an honest deployment map: the win is specific to consumer Ampere, and on A100 and B200 the same kernel loses to those cards' fast native bf16/FP8 paths.

</details>


### [88] [A Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Multi-Horizon Behavioural Forecasting for Mobile Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14604)
*Pavlos Nicolaou,Kleanthis Malialis,Artemis Kontou,Panayiotis Kolios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种深度学习架构、两种零样本基础模型（FM）及统计基线在可穿戴设备和智能手机行为时间序列预测上的表现，覆盖800多名参与者的步数、屏幕使用时间和睡眠时长数据，跨1-8天预测窗口进行评估。研究发现：无单一架构占优，PatchTST在训练模型中表现最佳；TimesFM在零样本场景下尤其是低数据条件下可与训练模型匹敌或超越；参与者级微调可降低RMSE 16-60%，睡眠改善最大而步数最小。这是首个联合评估现代深度学习、基础模型与个性化策略用于可穿戴设备多步行为预测的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备和智能手机产生丰富的行为时间序列数据，可支持主动健康干预，但尚缺乏对这些数据现代预测架构的系统比较，特别是在跨人群泛化、参与者级微调响应以及多日预测精度退化方面的研究空白。

Method: 在三个公共数据集（800+参与者）上，针对步数、屏幕时间和睡眠时长三个特征，使用1-8天预测窗口，基准测试六种深度学习架构（包括PatchTST、TCN、MLP、Transformer等）、两种零样本基础模型（TimesFM等）及统计基线，并开展跨所有六种架构的逐特征个性化研究，评估基础模型在不同数据集大小和时间粒度下的迁移能力。

Result: (i) 无单一架构全面占优，PatchTST在训练模型中领先，但前三名（TCN、MLP、Transformer）无显著差异；(ii) 基础模型TimesFM在零样本场景下匹配或超越训练模型，尤其在数据稀缺时；(iii) 参与者级微调使逐特征RMSE降低16-60%，睡眠预测改善最显著，步数改善最小。

Conclusion: 研究为移动健康预测中的架构选择、基础模型适用性和个性化策略提供了实践指导，是首个联合评估现代深度学习、基础模型与个性化方法用于可穿戴设备多步行为预测的工作。

Abstract: Wearable devices and smartphones generate rich behavioural time series that can support proactive health interventions, yet systematic comparisons of modern forecasting architectures for these data are lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how models generalise across populations, how different architectures respond to participant-level fine-tuning and how forecasting accuracy degrades across multi-day horizons. We benchmark six deep learning architectures, two zero-shot Foundation Models (FM) and statistical baselines on three public datasets encompassing over 800 participants, reporting per-feature metrics for step counts, screen time and sleep duration across 1-8 day horizons. We further conduct a per-feature personalisation study across all six architectures and assess FM transferability across dataset sizes and temporal granularities. Our key findings are: (i) no single architecture dominates, PatchTST leads among trained models while the three runners-up (TCN, MLP, Transformer) show no meaningful performance difference; (ii) the FM TimesFM matches or exceeds trained models zero-shot, especially in low-data regimes and (iii) participant-level fine-tuning reduces per-feature RMSE by 16-60\%, with sleep benefiting most and step counts least. These results provide practical guidance on architecture selection, FM applicability and personalisation strategies for mobile health forecasting. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to jointly evaluate modern deep learning, FMs and personalisation for multi-horizon behavioural forecasting from wearables.

</details>


### [89] [Expert-Driven Survival Machines: Improving Stratification and Interpretability in Multiple Clinical Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14608)
*Farica Zhuang,Zixuan Wen,Christos Davatzikos,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合专家模型的自适应深度聚类生存分析框架（AdaCSM），通过路由专家机制为不同患者子群动态分配专用风险预测器，在多个临床队列上提升了生存预测性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生存模型为所有患者学习统一特征表示，忽视了患者子群体之间的重要差异，导致个体化预测能力不足。

Method: 提出AdaCSM框架，引入基于路由的专家机制，在参数化生存建模框架中实现条件专业化，动态分配患者到专用风险预测器，同时优化生存预测和子类型聚类目标。

Result: 在多个真实世界纵向临床队列（覆盖多种疾病领域）上，与最先进的生存分析和深度聚类模型相比，AdaCSM展示了更优的预测性能和可解释的生存分析结果。

Conclusion: 混合专家机制能有效捕捉患者异质性生存模式，通过动态路由分配实现个体化风险分层，为精准医疗提供了可解释的生存分析工具。

Abstract: Survival prediction plays a central role for healthcare providers and clinical researchers. Accurate risk stratification enables early intervention and improved patient management. Most existing deep survival models learn one common feature representation for all patients, which may hide important differences between patient subgroups. In contrast, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework allows different parts of the model to focus on different patient patterns, leading to more individualized representations. Therefore, in this work, we propose a mixture-of-experts enhanced adaptive deep clustering survival framework (AdaCSM) for modeling such heterogeneous survival patterns. We introduce a routing-based expert mechanism that enables conditional specialization within a parametric survival modeling framework. The proposed architecture allocates patients to specialized risk predictors dynamically while preserving the patient survival and subtype clustering objectives. We compare our method with state-of-the-art survival and deep clustering models on multiple real-world longitudinal clinical cohorts spanning diverse disease domains. The proposed method demonstrates improved predictive performance and leads to interpretable results in survival analysis.

</details>


### [90] [Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14620)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测量了DiffusionGemma 26B模型的解码顺序，发现其既非并行也非块自回归，而是呈现从左到右的部分提交偏置，且"块大小"是测量尺度的伪影而非架构特性。模型以大批量同时提交令牌，内部顺序未定义；行为依赖任务类型。核心贡献是方法论：提出了测量解码顺序时必须处理的多个混杂因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放扩散语言模型被宣传为并行非自回归解码器，但实际检查点提交令牌的顺序从未被测量过，存在声称与实测之间的鸿沟。

Method: 对DiffusionGemma 26B模型进行仪器化，钩取其采样器的接受步骤，记录686个提示、六个场景下每个画布位置的提交时间与置信度。

Result: 解码呈现从左到右的偏置但令牌级顺序弱；块大小是测量粒度的伪影；模型以大批量同时提交，内部顺序未定义；结构化JSON以任意顺序提交；提交置信度与数学推理正确性相关但与事实回忆无关；提交激进，在步预算内短时爆发完成。

Conclusion: 测量解码顺序必须处理尾部EOS填充、场景内混杂、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性和大批量提交平局等混杂因素，否则会得到不存在的解码顺序结论。

Abstract: Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.

</details>


### [91] [Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14636)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie,Defu Lian,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性工具热流（A-IHF），一种用于灵活控制函数的确定性图扩散残差提取器。A-IHF将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号，通过 pilot 扩散检测处理变量的大跳跃并衰减跨跳跃的电导，利用稀疏图 resolvent 计算生成的控制函数。结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性及图可接受性滤波的选择规则，理论分解误差为结构泄漏、残差衰减和残差化处理变异，给出有限样本界、图可接受性率和有限路径选择校准。在54个合成基准单元中，受保护的观测A-IHF在结构响应MSE上平均最低，A-IHF家族在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制函数工具变量估计器需要一阶段残差而非仅一阶段预测，高容量一阶段可能插值处理变量，给结果方程留下过少残差信息。现有方法在处理非线性、高维及复杂数据结构时面临挑战，需要更有效的残差提取方法。

Method: 提出A-IHF方法，将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号，通过pilot扩散检测处理变量的跳跃，衰减跨跳跃的导纳，使用稀疏图resolvent计算生成的控制函数。选择规则结合GGCV、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可接受性滤波。理论分析分解误差为结构泄漏、残差衰减和残差化处理变异三个分量。

Result: 在54个合成基准单元中，受保护的观测A-IHF具有最低的平均结构响应MSE；A-IHF家族在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线（包括图、核、树、boosting、级数和神经网络控制函数基线）。当图能捕捉分段平滑的一阶段结构时性能最强。

Conclusion: A-IHF作为灵活控制函数的确定性图扩散残差提取器，在多种基准设置下优于现有控制函数方法，特别是在图能捕捉分段平滑的一阶段结构时表现最佳。该方法提供了完整的理论保证，包括有限样本界和图可接受性率。

Abstract: Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.

</details>


### [92] [Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14640)
*Simone Di Gregorio,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线凸优化（OCO）中引入带噪成对探测（pairwise probing）的模型。学习者有k≤T次探测预算，每次可查询两个点并获知哪个损失更小（可能带δ噪声）。主要结果表明，即使次线性且有噪声的探测预算也能显著改进最坏情况遗憾，达到了O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)})的遗憾界，并在T、k和δ上紧（对数因子除外）。当δ接近1/2（即硬币翻转）时，遗憾保证平滑退化。对d专家设置，所得速率在所有参数（包括d）上完全紧。分析通过方差缩减效应量化探测收益，并结合连续指数权重的二阶（方差基）分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一并泛化两个近期研究方向：专家设置中的次线性最优专家查询，以及OCO中每轮可用的成对（基于比较）反馈。旨在研究有限探测预算能否以及如何改善OCO的遗憾界。

Method: 1. 提出统一探测模型，学习者有k≤T次成对探测预算，每次可查询两个点并获知比较结果（可能带δ噪声）。2. 通过方差缩减效应量化探测收益。3. 结合连续指数权重的二阶（方差基）分析进行遗憾界推导。

Result: 在OCO全反馈设置下，带k次δ-噪声成对探测的遗憾界为：Reg_T ≤ O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)})，在T、k和δ上紧（对数因子除外）。对有限集K的d专家设置，所得速率在所有参数（包括d）上完全紧。

Conclusion: 证明了即使次线性且有噪声的成对探测预算也能在理论上改善OCO的最坏情况遗憾。遗憾随噪声参数δ平滑退化，当δ接近1/2时效果最弱。给出了统一的成对探测分析框架。

Abstract: We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO. In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss.
  Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$.
  Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.

</details>


### [93] [Which Directions Matter? Sparse Design for Affine Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14648)
*Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores,My Duong,Juan S. Borrero,Kaixun Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于有限字典和预算约束的鲁棒机器学习中不确定性集的选择问题，提出了一种基于覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则，并证明该目标是单调子模的，支持贪心方法并具有(1-1/e)近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒机器学习和优化依赖于不确定性模型的选择，但如何从有限字典和预算约束下选择合适的不确定性方向子集尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出原子不确定性集的概念（具有闭式支撑函数），设计基于覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则，覆盖方向包括梯度、对抗扰动或留出数据上的偏移。证明该目标函数具有单调性和子模性，从而支持贪心算法并获得(1-1/e)近似保证及匹配的硬性下限。同时提供损失边界证书和半径校准规则。

Result: 证明了覆盖目标是单调子模的，贪心方法具有(1-1/e)近似保证且边界不可改进；提供了所选子集损失的上界证书以及具有样本外控制的半径校准规则。

Conclusion: 该工作为数据驱动的鲁棒优化中不确定性集的选择提供了理论基础和实用算法，具有近似最优保证和统计控制。

Abstract: Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.

</details>


### [94] [Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14650)
*Christoph Bauschmann,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合图基因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法与泰勒函数近似的通用自适应策略，用于高效识别互联数据中的最优结构，并建立了次线性时间与线性数据量的理论性能保证，通过合成和真实交通数据集验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模互联数据中识别最优结构需要大量采样和计算资源，而学习利用非线性统计依赖关系虽能提升效率但复杂度高，亟需通用且自适应的策略来解决这一挑战。

Method: 开发了基于图因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法以及泰勒函数近似的通用自适应策略，并建立了次线性时间复杂度和线性数据量复杂度的理论性能保证，同时分析了噪声干扰、模型收敛和空间不匹配等不确定性下的鲁棒性。

Result: 理论分析表明所提策略在时间上为次线性、在数据量上为线性；数值实验在合成数据和真实交通数据集上验证了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架具有通用性，仅需极少前提条件或先验估计，并可通过多种修改适应特定或扩展场景。

Abstract: The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14662)
*Ines Nolasco,Jules Cauzinille,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Milad Alizadeh,Emmanuel Fernandez,Matthieu Geist,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Olivier Pietquin,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统揭示了预训练音频嵌入在生物声学中编码的声学特征，发现没有单一模型能覆盖全部88个eGeMAPS特征空间，拼接嵌入表现最佳（响度特征R²=0.76，F0最难恢复R²=0.33），并通过可恢复性与特征显著性交叉参考，提供了数据驱动的模型选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物声学领域广泛使用预训练音频嵌入，但缺乏对这些模型具体编码了哪些声学特征以及哪些特征对特定任务有用的理解，这阻碍了模型透明度，限制了向稀有物种或数据稀缺领域的扩展。

Method: 使用88个eGeMAPS声学特征，跨越六个分类群，应用线性和非线性回归探针（probing）量化每个模型捕获的声学属性，并通过互信息（NMI）计算每个物种的特征显著性，将可恢复性与特征显著性交叉参考。

Result: 确认了"没有免费午餐"模式——无单一模型能覆盖完整特征空间；拼接嵌入性能最高，表明模型间声学空间覆盖互补；响度特征编码最好（R²=0.76），基频F0最难恢复（R²=0.33）。

Conclusion: 通过交叉参考可恢复性与逐物种特征显著性（NMI），为生物声学任务提供了数据驱动的模型选择指导。

Abstract: Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.

</details>


### [96] [When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14668)
*Yining Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路由专用的双适配器知识编辑方法RoutEdit，通过相关性路由器决定是否激活编辑记忆，并分别使用编辑适配器和局部性适配器处理直接编辑和无关查询，在三个基准上取得了最优的概率偏好准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑系统需要更新选定事实的同时保持邻近但不相关的行为不变。现有方法主要关注如何写入编辑，但忽略了何时抑制编辑这一问题，本文正是在记忆辅助编辑场景下研究这一核心设计问题。

Method: 提出RoutEdit方法，包含一个相关性路由器（relevance router）来判断输入提示是否应接收编辑记忆。被路由到的提示使用编辑适配器（edit adapter）以偏好新对象；未被路由的非直接提示使用独立的局部性适配器（locality adapter）以保留或恢复原始对象偏好。这是一种路由专用的双适配器编辑器。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，RoutEdit在CF、ZSRE和MQuaKE三个基准上分别取得0.8180、0.8946和0.9922的最佳总体概率偏好准确率。在Qwen3-8B上同样表现最佳。路由器消融实验显示不同数据集的最佳记忆边界不同：CF适合词法神经网络路由器，ZSRE和MQuaKE适合BGE嵌入路由。模块消融表明性能提升主要来自编辑注入与离线抑制的分离，而非简单地增加LoRA容量。

Conclusion: 知识编辑的关键设计问题不仅是如何写入编辑，还包括何时抑制编辑。通过路由机制区分直接编辑和无关查询，并分别用不同适配器处理，可以有效提升编辑的精确性和局部性。

Abstract: Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.

</details>


### [97] [Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14673)
*Jai Bhagat,Sara Molas-Medina,Giorgi Giglemiani,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究压缩计算（CC）玩具模型是否属于叠加计算（computation in superposition）的实例。作者发现CC模型通过噪声残差流混合输入，对应标签中的非预期混合矩阵。将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项后，发现性能提升与混合矩阵的幅度呈正相关，移除该矩阵后性能消失。学习的神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间，表明混合项主导了解。基于混合矩阵的半非负矩阵分解（SNMF）基线重现了定性损失曲线并优于先前基线，但未达到训练模型性能。这些结果表明CC并非合适的叠加计算玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证Braun等人（2025）提出的压缩计算（CC）玩具模型是否真正体现了叠加计算（computation in superposition）的核心机制，即模型是否通过神经元的叠加表示来实现高效计算。

Method: 将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项，分析混合矩阵对模型性能的影响；通过特征值分解分析神经元方向与混合矩阵子空间的关系；设计基于混合矩阵的SNMF基线进行比较。

Result: CC模型的性能提升完全源于混合矩阵而非真正的叠加计算；移除混合矩阵后性能消失；神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间；SNMF基线能复现定性损失曲线但不如完整模型。

Conclusion: CC模型并非合适的叠加计算玩具模型，其性能主要由非预期的混合效应驱动，而非真正的计算叠加。

Abstract: We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.

</details>


### [98] [Flood and Harvest: The Provable Necessity of Trivia for Generating Valuable Mathematics via the Lens of Language Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14688)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Dai Shi,Zheng Gao,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在形式化证明验证器（如proof assistant）辅助下，数学内容生成的极限问题。将“有价值数学”建模为嵌套语言生成问题，证明了：验证器不能替代品味（无法识别数学价值）；验证器能确保覆盖未见过的有价值陈述，但代价是生成无限多“正确但无价值”的琐碎陈述；这些琐碎陈述的数量存在一个尖锐二分法——若只允许有限个琐碎陈述，最优覆盖率为α/2；若允许无限个（即使比例趋于零），最优覆盖率跳升至1-α/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统结合proof assistant可以大规模生成形式化数学，但验证器能验证的内容与数学家认为有价值的内容之间存在巨大差距。这一差距已成为制约AI数学发展的关键瓶颈。本文旨在从理论层面刻画这一差距的本质，回答"验证器能否替代品味"等根本性问题。

Method: 将问题建模为嵌套语言生成问题：形式化可验证语言F（通过成员查询预言机访问）中包含未知的有价值语言H，H的核心C以精确密度α通过对抗性枚举呈现（代表已有文献）。每个输出分为三类：有价值的（∈H）、琐碎的（∈F\H）、幻觉（∉F）。在此框架下从计算学习理论角度分析生成系统的极限性质。

Result: 回答了四个问题：(1) 验证器不能替代品味，可实现的广度生成类与无验证器模型相同；(2) 验证器能确保覆盖所有未见过的有价值陈述，同时只断言有效陈述，将不可避免的错误从错误断言转移到琐碎断言上；(3) 存在尖锐二分法：有限琐碎陈述下最优覆盖率为α/2，无限琐碎陈述（即使速率趋零）下最优覆盖率跳升至1-α/2；(4) 两个 regime 都在数学的压缩模型中有实现。

Conclusion: 完美的验证器无法替代数学品味：无限的正确但无价值的陈述流不是工程意外，而是可证明的理论必然性——因为覆盖未记录的有价值数学内容必然需要无限但渐近可忽略的已认证琐碎陈述流。

Abstract: AI systems coupled to proof assistants now generate formal mathematics at scale, and the gap between what a checker can verify and what a mathematician would value has become the binding constraint. We model the generation of valuable mathematics as nested language generation in the limit: a verifiable formal language $F$, accessed through a membership oracle (the proof checker), contains an unknown valuable language $H \in \mathcal{H}$ revealed only through an adversarial enumeration of a core $C \subseteq H$ of exact density $α$ (the literature). Every output is valuable ($\in H$), trivial ($\in F \setminus H$), or a hallucination ($\notin F$). We settle four questions. First, the verifier is not taste: the collections admitting generation with breadth are exactly those of the oracle-free model, characterized fiber-wise by Angluin's condition. Second, the verifier does buy sound coverage, covering all unseen valuable statements while asserting only valid ones: possible with it, impossible without it; it relocates unavoidable errors from false to trivial. Third, and centrally, a sharp dichotomy on the tight family: generators emitting finitely many trivia achieve optimal coverage $α/2$, while any infinite trivia allowance, even at vanishing rate, jumps the optimum to $1-α/2$ (both tight, for cores presented as the candidate intersection), and one generator attains both ends. The transition is in trivia count, not rate; the gap $1-α$ is the unrecorded mass. Fourth, both regimes instantiate in a compression model of mathematics. A perfect verifier cannot substitute for taste: the unbounded stream of correct-but-worthless statements is not an engineering accident but a provable necessity, since covering unrecorded valuable mathematics requires an infinite, but asymptotically negligible, stream of certified trivia.

</details>


### [99] [A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14690)
*Abdellah Aznag,Rachel Cummings,Adam N. Elmachtoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多组均值估计d臂老虎机中的最大风险主动学习目标，提出了首个局部极小极大下界，将难度分解为预算项、异方差指数和方差局部曲率三个正交因子，并通过损失诱导的ℓ1几何和基于表示的实例生成器证明了近优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习理论缺乏对最坏情况不确定性（max-risk）目标的系统下界分析，尤其是针对异方差多组设定。

Method: 建立局部极小极大框架；引入损失诱导的ℓ1几何和基于表示的实例生成器，将困难实例构造转化为显式随机矩阵计算。

Result: 首次给出该目标的一般下界，证明难度分解为预算项、异方差指数和VLC三项；VLC在光滑类中是方差-费雪信息量的重参数化，且具有闭式解。

Conclusion: 与最强上界对比显示在广泛范围内近优（对数因子内），但在高度异质实例中存在系统性差距。

Abstract: We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.

</details>


### [100] [Persona-Pruner: Sculpting Lightweight Models for Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14695)
*Jinsu Kim,Jihoon Tack,Noah Lee,Jongheon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Persona-Pruner框架，通过从大型语言模型中提取与特定角色相关的子网络，构建轻量级角色扮演模型，在大幅降低计算成本的同时保留角色扮演能力，相比现有剪枝方法性能下降减少93.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型作为角色扮演聊天机器人时，为每个角色分配完整通用模型导致计算成本过高，在需要大量NPC同时交互的真实应用场景中效率低下。

Method: 提出Persona-Pruner框架，从单个角色描述中分离出角色特定的子网络，而非直接进行剪枝，从而区分冗余知识与必需的角色特质。

Result: 在RoleBench基准上，Persona-Pruner相比现有最先进的LLM剪枝技术，将密集模型的性能下降减少最高93.8%（以LLM-as-a-judge评分衡量），同时保持通用LLM能力。

Conclusion: 角色身份仅依赖模型总容量的一小部分，通过精心提取角色特定子网络可以有效构建轻量级角色扮演模型，无需为每个角色保留完整通用模型。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) have shown remarkable potential as role-playing chatbots, delivering consistent, stylized interactions when given a specification of a character or user persona. However, applying these capabilities to real-world applications (e.g., ecosystems with numerous NPCs interacting simultaneously) exposes a critical inefficiency due to the excessive computational cost. In this paper, we question the necessity of dedicating a full, generalist model to a single persona, hypothesizing that a specific character identity relies on only a fraction of the model's total capacity. We observe that naively pruning LMs often severely degrades the role-playing performance for a specific persona; it does not distinguish between redundant knowledge and essential character traits. We propose Persona-Pruner, a framework that sculpts a lightweight role-playing model by isolating persona-specific sub-networks from a single description. Our experiments consistently show that Persona-Pruner preserves role-playing performance substantially more effectively than existing state-of-the-art LLM pruning techniques, reducing the performance drop from the dense model by up to 93.8% over the strongest baseline on RoleBench in LLM-as-a-judge score, while still maintaining general LLM capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Learning Dynamic Swing-Up of an Inverted Pendulum using Remote Magnetic Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13915)
*Viacheslav Sydora,Jasan Zughaibi,Denis von Arx,Quentin Boehler,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用临床级Navion电磁导航系统，首次实现了磁驱动倒立摆的摆起控制。该方法结合轨迹优化、时变LQR状态反馈和迭代学习控制（ILC），在六次迭代内成功完成摆起。实验表明，ILC学习的校正量与高保真磁场模型校准预测的力矩偏差高度吻合，验证了学习与自适应方法处理电磁驱动中不确定性的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电磁导航系统（eMNS）研究多依赖准静态控制，动态方法虽已展现优势，但远离平衡态的轨迹跟踪问题仍未得到充分解决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出结合轨迹优化（考虑eMNS内部动力学）、时变LQR状态反馈和迭代学习控制（ILC）的混合控制方法，利用先前试验数据与系统动态模型逐步优化前馈指令。

Result: 单独使用LQR失败，而ILC在六次迭代内成功完成摆起。ILC学习的校正量与高保真磁场模型校准预测的力矩偏差高度吻合。

Conclusion: 学习与自适应方法是处理电磁驱动中不确定性（如患者特异性生理运动模式和磁场模型校准误差）的有效工具，且该方法可推广至导管、导丝等磁驱动器械。

Abstract: Electromagnetic Navigation Systems (eMNS) have gained considerable attention for minimally invasive surgery and targeted drug delivery. While most of the literature relies on quasi-static control of these systems, recent work has demonstrated the benefits of dynamic approaches. However, trajectory tracking far from equilibrium states remains largely unaddressed. We close this gap by demonstrating the first swing-up of a magnetically actuated inverted pendulum using the clinically-ready Navion eMNS. Although the inverted pendulum is not clinically relevant in itself, the proposed method utilizes torques and forces as control objectives, making it applicable to other magnetically actuated devices such as catheters and guidewires. Our approach combines trajectory optimization that accounts for internal eMNS dynamics with time-varying Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) state feedback and Iterative Learning Control (ILC), which leverages previous trial data and the system's dynamic model to progressively refine the feedforward command. While LQR alone fails due to the complex phenomena of magnetic actuation, ILC enables successful swing-up within six iterations. Furthermore, post-experimental analysis reveals that the learned ILC correction closely matches the torque discrepancy predicted by high-fidelity magnetic field model calibration, suggesting learning and adaptation as a promising tool to deal with uncertainties in electromagnetic actuation arising, e.g., from patient-specific physiological motion patterns and field model calibration inaccuracies.

</details>


### [102] [Occupancy-Grounded Room Segmentation for Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13727)
*Carlos Cueto Zumaya,Iacopo Catalano,Jorge Peña-Queralta,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于占用分解的层次化3D场景图构建方法，将房间节点锚定在可追踪的自由空间区域上，生成显式的多边形房间足迹，并在Matterport3D数据集上与Hydra基线进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图的房间层构建依赖不同的空间基元（如位置聚类、墙壁平面或分割输出），导致房间节点缺乏统一的几何评价标准，无法进行公平比较。

Method: 提出占用接地（occupancy-grounded）的3DSG管线，通过占用分解（occupancy decomposition）追踪自由空间区域，将房间节点锚定在这些区域上，并为每个房间生成显式的多边形足迹（polygonal footprint）。

Result: 在12个Matterport3D场景上评估，占用接地的锚定方法比Hydra（基于位置连接性的基线）恢复了显著更多的房间实例，但精度较低；两种方法在墙壁精确的房间边界问题上仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: 占用接地方法能更有效地恢复房间实例，但精度有待提升；房间边界的精确估计仍是开放问题；代码已开源。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs (3DSGs) for indoor robots organize geometric and semantic information across spatial scales, with a room layer that connects object-level perception to room-scale reasoning. Existing systems construct this layer from different spatial substrates (\eg{} place clusters, wall planes, or segmentation outputs), and as a result, room nodes are not evaluated on a common geometric criterion. We present an occupancy-grounded 3DSG pipeline in which room nodes are anchored to tracked free-space regions derived from occupancy decomposition, giving each room an explicit polygonal footprint. We evaluate the pipeline on 12 Matterport3D scenes by matching predicted room polygons to annotated room instances and compare against Hydra, a representative state-of-the-art place-connectivity baseline. The results show that occupancy-grounded anchoring recovers substantially more room instances than place-connectivity construction, at the cost of lower precision, and that wall-accurate room boundaries remain an open problem for both methods. Code is available at https://github.com/crcz25/OccuSG.

</details>


### [103] [Scalable Dynamic Tactile Sensing Enabled by Passive and Flexible Acoustic Waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13746)
*Guimin Long,Changhong Linghu,Chuanping Liu,Ke Xu,Xingjian Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深亚波长声波导的被动分布式触觉感知范式，通过弹性膜封装的亥姆霍兹谐振器和弹簧增强微管网络实现弯曲不变性，仅用4个麦克风即可实现64节点阵列的高精度触觉定位（4mm分辨率，>99%准确率）和低频信号波形重建，并展示了指尖阵列、触觉手套和大面积皮肤等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感技术在扩展到大面积阵列时面临灵敏度、鲁棒性、柔顺性之间的权衡，且布线复杂、成本高昂，亟需一种可扩展、低成本且性能不退化的解决方案。

Method: 采用深亚波长声波导（弹性膜封装的亥姆霍兹谐振器 + 弹簧增强微管网络）构建被动分布式传感系统，通过稀疏嵌入麦克风捕获声学信号，结合快速连续小波变换和轻量级神经网络实现实时触觉定位与波形重建。

Result: 4个麦克风驱动64节点阵列时达到4mm空间分辨率和>99%定位准确率；推理时间仅5.5ms；可检测从单根毛发接触到5mg颗粒冲击、动脉脉搏波、羽毛触碰和手指接触等多样刺激。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种可扩展、柔性、低成本的新型触觉感知范式，为下一代人机界面提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Artificial dynamic tactile sensing requires sensitivity, robustness, and compliance, yet existing technologies face trade-offs when scaling to large-area arrays, compounded by wiring complexity and cost. Here, we report a passive distributed paradigm using deep sub-wavelength acoustic waveguides that decouples performance from structural flexibility. Elastic-membrane-capped Helmholtz resonators interconnected by spring-reinforced microtubes form an enclosed network with invariant acoustic transmission under macroscopic bending. By sparsely embedding microphones, the system achieves real-time localization (4 mm highest spatial resolution; >99% accuracy in a 4 microphones 64-node sensing array) and waveform reconstruction of low-frequency signals (<100 Hz). Fast Continuous Wavelet Transform and a lightweight neural network enable inference within 5.5 ms. We demonstrate conformable prototypes-fingertip arrays, a tactile glove, and large-area skins-detecting stimuli from single-hair contact to 5-mg particle impacts, arterial pulse waves, feather touches, and finger contact. This establishes a scalable, flexible, low-cost paradigm for next-generation human-machine interfaces.

</details>


### [104] [$μ_0$: A Scalable 3D Interaction-Trace World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13769)
*Seungjae Lee,Yoonkyo Jung,Jusuk Lee,Jonghun Shin,Amir Hossein Shahidzadeh,Yao-Chih Lee,H. Jin Kim,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出$μ_0$，一种基于3D轨迹的可扩展世界模型。它通过预测交互点的平滑3D轨迹（而非像素或直接动作）作为紧凑、无关具身形态的运动接口。配合TraceExtract系统从多样化视频中自动提取3D监督信号进行预训练，冻结后的$μ_0$可配合动作专家用于下游机器人具身形态，实现与有动作监督的VLA模型相竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型要么在像素空间预测视频（浪费容量在密集外观重建上），要么直接预测动作（需要具身形态特定的动作标签，难以扩展）。作者希望找到一种既紧凑、又无关具身形态、可从多样化视频源中学习的统一运动表示。

Method: 1) 提出$μ_0$模型，预测交互点的3D轨迹（用B样条控制点表示），而非像素或动作。2) 设计TraceExtract系统，自动从视频中提取关键点、构建全局对齐的3D轨迹、用层次化语言描述关联运动片段。3) 采用预训练的视觉-语言骨干网络结合模块化轨迹专家（Trace Expert）进行预训练。4) 冻结$μ_0$后，为不同机器人具身形态配对动作专家（trace-conditioned policies）。

Result: 在2D和3D轨迹预测任务上，$μ_0$优于基线方法（包括轨迹预测模型和tokenized VLM方法）。下游实验中，基于$μ_0$冻结表示训练的轨迹条件策略，虽然在动作无关预训练下，性能与有动作监督预训练的VLA模型（如$π_0$）具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 3D轨迹是一种可扩展、可迁移的跨具身形态操作表示，能够在不需要动作标签的情况下从大规模视频中学习，并有效迁移到下游机器人控制。

Abstract: World models that capture how actions induce physical change enable scalable robot learning without reliance on embodiment-specific action labels. Pixel-space video models provide broad visual priors but expend model capacity on dense appearance reconstruction, while direct action models require embodiment-specific labels that hinder scalability. We present $μ_0$, a scalable world model based on 3D traces. Rather than predicting dense pixels or directly modeling actions, $μ_0$ forecasts smooth 3D trajectories for salient interaction points such as objects, tools, hands, and contact regions, yielding a compact, embodiment-agnostic motion interface. To enable training from diverse video sources, our TraceExtract system automatically extracts 3D supervision by selecting keypoints, constructing globally aligned traces, and associating motion segments with hierarchical language captions. This TraceExtract supervision pretrains $μ_0$ by combining a pretrained vision-language backbone with a modular trace expert, which represents each query via B-spline control points and predicts future traces. Experiments show that $μ_0$ outperforms baselines in both 2D and 3D trace prediction, including trace prediction models and tokenized VLM methods. Because $μ_0$ is frozen and reusable, it can be paired with action experts for downstream robot embodiments. Despite action-free pretraining, the resulting trace-conditioned policies achieve performance competitive with VLA models pretrained with action supervision, such as $π_0$. These results establish 3D traces as a scalable and transferable representation for cross-embodiment manipulation.

</details>


### [105] [Semidefinite Relaxations for Collision-Free Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14063)
*Bernhard Paus Graesdal,Alexandre Amice,Pablo A. Parrilo,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了无碰撞运动规划的半定松弛方法，提出了一个自然半定松弛并给出了两项关键理论见解：一是证明了凸松弛等价于在更高维空间中求解全局最优的运动规划问题，提供了紧性的充要条件；二是证明了松弛具有对称性约简，使半定锥尺寸仅随多项式次数线性增长而与环境维度无关。该方法比SNOPT和IPOPT快10-100倍，并可作为RRT规划器的凸导向函数用于四旋翼轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无碰撞运动规划问题的半定松弛缺乏严格的理论分析，其紧性条件和计算效率问题尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 将无碰撞运动规划建模为多项式曲线上的非凸问题，提出自然半定松弛；通过几何解释揭示凸松弛与高维空间运动规划的等价性；利用对称性约简降低半定锥尺寸。

Result: 半定松弛比SNOPT和IPOPT快10-100倍，求解时间方差更低，能可靠找到局部最优路径；作为RRT凸导向函数有效用于C^4连续的四旋翼最小snap规划。

Conclusion: 首次对无碰撞运动规划的半定松弛进行了理论分析，给出了紧性的几何解释和充要条件，并利用对称性约简大幅提升计算效率。

Abstract: We study semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. We focus on a point robot moving from start to goal through spherical obstacles in $\mathbb{R}^n$, subject to path continuity constraints and squared derivative costs; a setting that is conceptually simple yet captures the hardness of collision-free motion planning. We formulate this problem exactly as a nonconvex problem over polynomial curves, and present a natural semidefinite relaxation. We contribute two key theoretical insights; to our knowledge this is the first theoretical analysis of semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. First, we show that solving the convex relaxation is equivalent to solving, to global optimality, a related motion planning problem in a potentially higher-dimensional space. This geometric interpretation yields necessary and sufficient conditions for tightness, and a clear intuition for when the relaxation is loose. Second, we show that the relaxation admits a symmetry reduction that makes it significantly smaller than one might expect, with positive semidefinite cone sizes that scale linearly with the polynomial degree and are independent of the ambient dimension. The resulting relaxation is 10 to 100 times faster than direct nonlinear programming transcriptions solved with SNOPT and IPOPT, exhibits significantly lower variance in solve times, and reliably finds a locally optimal path for the original problem. We demonstrate its effectiveness as a convex steering function in an RRT planner for minimum-snap quadrotor planning with $C^4$ continuous trajectories.

</details>


### [106] [FlowMo-WM: A World Model with Object Momentum and Hidden Ambient Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13817)
*Yitao Jiang,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowMo-WM是一个端到端可训练的世界模型，通过将图像动作历史分解为短历史隐状态（物体运动）和长历史上下文（隐藏漂移），在无需流场直接监督的情况下提升水下表面车辆在隐藏水流等漂移环境中的长期滚动预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型多假设运动受即时控制主导，而水下表面车辆等真实物体受惯性和隐藏环境漂移（如水流水流、风力）影响，现有模型难以准确预测此类场景下的长期状态。

Method: 提出FlowMo-WM，将图像动作历史分解为短历史隐状态（总结物体中心运动）和长历史上下文（总结慢变外生影响）；设计零上下文残差过渡机制，在隐空间滚动中分离动作条件基础动力学与上下文依赖的漂移效应。

Result: 在具有多样化隐藏流、扰动和随机车辆动力学的模拟水生表面车辆环境中，FlowMo-WM在长期滚动预测精度上优于代表性动作条件隐空间世界模型。上下文消融实验（零化或打乱上下文）表明环境上下文对稳定预测至关重要，冻结线性探针表征了所学因子的信息编码。

Conclusion: FlowMo-WM通过显式建模隐藏漂移上下文，有效提升了存在惯性及环境漂移场景下的世界模型预测能力，无需直接监督流场信息。

Abstract: World models in robot learning predict future states from visual observations and actions, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of their controls. However, many action-conditioned models are evaluated in settings where motion is dominated by immediate control, whereas aquatic surface vehicles and other real-world objects continue moving under inertia and are displaced by hidden ambient drift, such as water currents or wind. We propose FlowMo-WM, an end-to-end trainable visual world model that infers object-centric motion state and a predictive long-history context associated with hidden drift from image-action histories without direct supervision of flow fields. FlowMo-WM factorizes image-action history into a short-history latent state, trained to summarize object-centric motion, and a longer-history context, trained to summarize slowly varying exogenous influences. A zero-context residual transition separates action-conditioned base dynamics from context-dependent drift effects during latent rollout. In simulated aquatic surface-vehicle environments with diverse hidden flows, disturbances, and randomized vehicle dynamics, FlowMo-WM improves long-horizon rollout accuracy over representative action-conditioned latent world models. Prediction-time context ablations, in which the inferred context is zeroed or shuffled during rollout, show that the ambient context is important for stable prediction under hidden drift, while frozen linear probes characterize information encoded in the learned factors.

</details>


### [107] [Robustness without Wrinkles: Parallel Simulation and Robust MPC for Certified Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14188)
*Wei-Chen Li,Jeffrey Fang,Sasanka Polisetti,Yuexi Song,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORD-SLS是一种基于GPU并行可微模拟器的实时控制方法，通过输出反馈鲁棒MPC和共形预测校准实现绳索和布料的安全、高速操作，在多种任务中超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决可变形物体（绳索和布料）在接触丰富的环境中安全、实时操作的问题，现有方法在模型不确定性和感知误差下难以保证安全约束。

Method: 提出GPU并行可微模拟器（含接触平滑）实现基于梯度的快速规划；开发实时GPU并行输出反馈鲁棒MPC算法，结合共形预测校准视觉反馈和感知误差边界，生成可达管以实现高概率安全控制。

Result: 在仿真和硬件上的高维接触丰富绳索/布料任务（避障、布线、折叠、平滑）中，达到毫秒级规划速度，在安全性、速度和任务成功率上均超越基线。

Conclusion: CORD-SLS有效实现了可变形物体的实时安全操控，其GPU并行可微模拟器和鲁棒MPC框架具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: We present CORD-SLS, a real-time control method for safe deformable object manipulation, with a focus on ropes and cloth. At its core is a GPU-parallel differentiable simulator with contact smoothing which enables efficient gradient-based planning through intermittent contact. To robustly satisfy constraints under model and sensing uncertainty, we develop a real-time, GPU-parallel output-feedback robust model predictive control (MPC) algorithm that plans with this simulator. We further show that the simulator accelerates model-based RL for training neural manipulation policies. To improve real-world robustness, we use conformal prediction to calibrate visual-feedback and perception-error bounds for MPC, producing reachable tubes that enable high-probability safe control. We evaluate CORD-SLS on high-dimensional, contact-rich rope and cloth manipulation tasks in simulation and hardware, including obstacle avoidance, routing, folding, and smoothing. Across settings, CORD-SLS achieves millisecond-speed planning, exceeding baselines in safety, speed, and task success.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Agent Embodied Autonomous Driving: From V2X Information Exchange to Shared World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13840)
*Senkang Hu,Zhengru Fang,Yihang Tao,Zihan Fang,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了自动驾驶从单车智能向多智能体具身系统（MAEAD）的转变，以共享世界模型（SWMs）为核心视角，回顾了380余篇文献，涵盖V2X通信、协同感知、认知推理、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶及闭环仿真验证等领域，指出当前评估集中于仿真和离线协议，基础模型协调缺乏实时安全保证，并提出了可验证共享状态维护、意图与计划对齐、以及通信/延迟/部署约束下的安全协调行动等关键研究优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶正从孤立车辆智能向多智能体具身系统演进，需要在不确定性下共享感知、推断意图并协调行动。现有研究分散且缺乏统一理论框架，亟需从共享世界模型视角系统审视该领域的发展现状与关键挑战。

Method: 以共享世界模型（SWMs）为组织框架，对380余篇文献进行系统综述，覆盖V2X通信、协同感知、智能体间认知、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶及闭环仿真与数据引擎等研究方向，分析从观测对齐到状态统一、意图感知交互再到协调行动的技术链条。

Result: 当前评估仍集中于仿真环境、标准基准和离线协议；基于基础模型的协调缺乏在开放交通场景下可验证的实时安全保证；提出多智能体具身自动驾驶（MAEAD）的三项核心研究优先级。

Conclusion: 共享世界模型为多智能体协同驾驶提供了统一视角，但现有工作在可验证共享状态维护、鲁棒的意图与计划对齐、以及在通信、延迟和部署约束下的安全协调行动方面仍存在显著缺口，需作为未来重点研究方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is shifting from isolated vehicle intelligence toward multi-agent embodied systems that share perception, infer intent, and coordinate action under uncertainty. This survey examines this transition through the lens of Shared World Models (SWMs): predictive cross-agent representations maintained across vehicles, infrastructure, and other traffic participants. We review more than 380 publications spanning vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, collaborative perception, inter-agent cognition, cooperative planning, end-to-end cooperative driving, and simulation and data engines for closed-loop validation. The organizing question is how exchanged observations become aligned state, intent-aware interaction, and coordinated downstream action. Across the surveyed literature, evaluation remains concentrated in simulation, curated benchmarks, and offline protocols. Foundation-model-based coordination also lacks verified real-time safety guarantees in open traffic. These gaps motivate key research priorities for multi-agent embodied autonomous driving (MAEAD): verifiable shared-state maintenance, robust intent and plan alignment, and safe coordinated action under communication, latency, and deployment constraints.

</details>


### [109] [Short-Horizon Position Accuracy of Single-Track Models: Implications for Motion Planning of Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14216)
*Aron J. Aertssen,Lars A. T. H. van Alen,Igo J. M. Besselink,Rudolf G. M. Huisman,René M. J. G. van de Molengraft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种单轨车辆模型在多种驾驶工况下的短时域位置精度，通过实测数据参数化模型，揭示了模型复杂度、参数化质量与位置精度之间的权衡关系，为模型预测控制中的模型选择提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆模型的短时域位置精度缺乏基于实测数据的系统评估，而位置精度直接影响自动驾驶轨迹规划的可行性与安全性。

Method: 采用三种单轨车辆模型，通过专用实验对测试车辆进行参数辨识，在不同驾驶工况下将模型预测结果与实测数据进行对比，评估短时域位置精度。

Result: 不同模型在位置精度上存在差异，精度受到模型复杂度与参数化质量的共同影响，并非越复杂的模型精度越高。

Conclusion: 本文不寻求确定单一最优模型，而是为模型预测控制应用中的模型选择提供关于复杂度、参数化质量与位置精度之间权衡的深入理解。

Abstract: Accurate and computationally efficient vehicle models are essential for motion planning of autonomous vehicles, where positional accuracy directly affects trajectory feasibility and safety. However, the positional accuracy has not been systematically evaluated against real measurements. Therefore, this paper compares the short-horizon positional accuracy of three single-track vehicle models against vehicle measurements across various driving maneuvers. Model parameters are identified through dedicated experiments with the instrumented test vehicle. Rather than identifying a single best model, this work aims to provide insight into the trade-offs between model complexity, parameterization quality, and positional accuracy for informed model selection in Model Predictive Control applications.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient Domain-Adaptive Policy Learning via Kernel Representation with Application to Quadrotor Control under Non-Stationary Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13842)
*Hongyu Zhou,Mingtian Tan,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于核表示的高效域自适应策略学习算法，通过随机傅里叶特征对未知扰动进行可微核近似，在离线训练中随机采样核系数和带宽参数生成多样化扰动分布，并通过可微仿真优化策略；在线部署时通过最小二乘估计实时更新核参数，实现了四旋翼无人机在复杂扰动下的自适应轨迹跟踪，训练仅需50秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域自适应策略学习面临环境表示既要足够表达复杂的仿真到现实差距，又要在部署时支持快速在线适应的双重挑战，例如四旋翼可能遇到时变非平稳扰动（突风、负载变化、地面效应等）。

Method: 使用基于随机傅里叶特征的可微核近似建模未知扰动；离线阶段随机采样核系数和带宽生成多样化扰动分布，通过可微仿真和解析梯度优化控制策略（RTX 4090上仅需50秒）；在线部署时通过在线最小二乘估计同时更新核系数和带宽实现实时自适应。

Result: 在高保真数值仿真和Crazyflie硬件实验中，方法在包含复杂气动效应、风扰、地面效应和负载波动的各种扰动下均实现了有效的轨迹跟踪自适应。

Conclusion: 所提算法能够高效地将离线训练与在线自适应结合，在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中有效应对各类非平稳扰动，且训练效率极高。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for efficient domain-adaptive policy learning via kernel representations. Learning domain-adaptive policies is challenging since it requires an environment representation that is both sufficiently expressive to model complex sim-to-real gaps during offline training, and computationally efficient enough to support rapid online adaptation during deployment. For instance, a quadrotor may encounter time-varying, non-stationary disturbances, such as sudden gusts of wind, payload shifts, or transitions between distinct flight regimes with and without ground effects. To address these challenges, we model unknown disturbances using a differentiable kernel approximation based on random Fourier features. During the offline training phase, we randomly sample kernel coefficients and bandwidth parameters to generate a rich diversity of disturbance profiles. We then optimize the control policy via differentiable simulation with analytical gradients, a process that takes only 50 seconds of training time on an RTX 4090 GPU. During hardware deployment, the policy adapts to non-stationary environments in real time by updating both the kernel coefficients and bandwidth through online least-squares estimation. We evaluate our method on quadrotor trajectory tracking tasks across high-fidelity numerical simulations and hardware experiments using Crazyflie, subjected to various disturbances, including complex aerodynamic effects, wind, ground effects, and payload fluctuations.

</details>


### [111] [Output-Level Regularization Eliminates the Seed Lottery in Single-GPU VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13856)
*Jeffrin Sam,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-JEPA微调存在“种子彩票”现象：相同代码、相同数据、不同随机种子运行13次，12次成功（91-94%），1次性能骤降至65.2%且无任何警告。原因是输出坍缩——动作预测器忽略视觉输入产生几乎相同的输出。现有权重级正则化方法（L2、EWC）因雅可比零空间问题无法检测此坍缩，而输出级正则化方法（VICReg、Dropout、降低学习率）可完全消除灾难性种子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示并解决视觉-语言-动作模型微调中由随机种子导致的高方差性能退化问题，即“种子彩票”现象，以及现有权重级正则化方法无法检测输出坍缩的根本原因。

Method: 1. 系统性地在3个LIBERO基准上运行7种方法×最多13个种子；2. 通过雅可比零空间形式化分析输出坍缩与权重级正则化的盲区；3. 对比权重级（L2、EWC）和输出级（VICReg、Dropout、降低学习率）正则化方法的效果。

Result: 输出级正则化方法（VICReg 12种子、Dropout 4种子、半学习率5种子）完全消除了灾难性种子（0/21坍缩 vs 基准线1/13；F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001），而权重级方法（L2、EWC）无法解决种子彩票问题。

Conclusion: 最简单的修复方案是修改优化器配置中的一个数值（降低学习率），输出级正则化比权重级正则化更有效对抗输出坍缩。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language-action model (VLA-JEPA) on a single GPU should be simple: load a pretrained checkpoint, run training, deploy. There is a hidden danger. Run the same fine-tuning code thirteen times -- same data, same architecture, different random seed -- and twelve runs produce a robot succeeding 91--94% of the time, while one run silently degrades to 65.2%: a 29 pp gap with no error message, no warning, and no way to predict which seed will fail. We call this the seed lottery. We trace the cause to output collapse: the action predictor quietly learns to produce nearly identical outputs regardless of what the robot sees. Existing weight-level methods (L2, EWC) are structurally blind to this collapse -- they penalize weight changes, but collapse occurs in directions weights can move freely without affecting outputs, a gap we formalize via the Jacobian null-space. Across 7 methods x up to 13 seeds x 3 LIBERO benchmarks, three output-level regularizers -- VICReg (n=12 seeds), Dropout (n=4), and a halved learning rate (n=5) -- each eliminate every catastrophic seed (0/21 combined collapses vs. 1/13 Baseline; F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001), while weight-level methods (L2, EWC) preserve the lottery. The simplest fix is changing one number in your optimizer config.

</details>


### [112] [ForestBack: Breadcrumb-Based Pedestrian Dead Reckoning for Infrastructure-Free Return Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14421)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut,Chanakan Chaipan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForestBack是一个基于面包屑式行人航位推算(PDR)的无基础设施行人返航导航框架，通过记录行走路径为可逆面包屑节点并生成反向路径引导，在GPS拒止环境中实现可靠导航。实验表明，相比传统PDR，其均方根误差降低15.76%，最终位置误差从1.781米降至1.388米，转向事件检测一致性达99.90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止环境中（如森林、室内），缺乏可靠的外部定位基础设施，传统行人返航导航面临挑战。现有方法依赖Wi-Fi、蓝牙信标等基础设施，在无部署环境中不可用。因此，需要一种完全无基础设施的行人返航导航方案，仅利用便携设备内置传感器实现路径记录与反向引导。

Method: ForestBack框架基于面包屑式行人航位推算(PDR)，包含以下核心模块：(1) 基于加速度的步态检测；(2) 自适应步长估计；(3) 磁力计辅助航向估计；(4) 气压计高度校正；(5) 双向面包屑路径重建。系统将用户行走路径记录为可逆面包屑节点序列，据此生成反向路径引导。

Result: 在室内避障路线（5个检查点，36次步行试验，42,474条时间序列样本）上评估：ForestBack将平均RMSE从1.129米降至0.965米（改进15.76%）；平均最终位置误差从1.781米降至1.388米；转向事件检测一致性达99.90%。

Conclusion: ForestBack在避障场景中显著提升了轨迹重建和路径保持返回引导的性能，是一种有效的无基础设施PDR返航导航方案。已公开数据集和分析笔记本支持未来基准测试和可重复性研究。

Abstract: Reliable return navigation remains an important challenge in GPS-denied environments where external positioning infrastructure may be unavailable or unreliable. This paper presents ForestBack, an infrastructure-free pedestrian return navigation framework based on breadcrumb-based pedestrian dead reckoning (PDR). The system records a user's walking route as a sequence of reversible breadcrumb nodes and generates reverse-path guidance without requiring GPS, Wi-Fi, Bluetooth beacons, or pre-installed infrastructure. ForestBack integrates acceleration-based step detection, adaptive step-length estimation, magnetometer-assisted heading estimation, barometric-altitude correction, and bidirectional breadcrumb path reconstruction. The system was evaluated using an indoor obstacle-avoidance route with five checkpoints, where the user navigated around a central obstacle. A dataset of 36 walking trials and 42,474 time-series samples was used for evaluation, including IMU signals, magnetometer readings, barometric variables, turn-event labels, ground-truth trajectories, baseline PDR outputs, proposed ForestBack outputs, and power-related measurements. Experimental results show that ForestBack reduced the mean RMSE from 1.129 m to 0.965 m compared with traditional PDR, corresponding to a 15.76% improvement. The mean final-position error was reduced from 1.781 m to 1.388 m, while turn-event detection consistency reached approximately 99.90%. These results indicate that ForestBack improves trajectory reconstruction and route-preserving return guidance in obstacle-avoidance scenarios. The released dataset and analysis notebook support reproducibility and future benchmarking of infrastructure-free PDR-based return navigation systems.

</details>


### [113] [ContactWorld: What Matters in Vision-Tactile World Models for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13877)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Pokuang Zhou,Kaidi Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Temitope Amosa,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Jiuzhou Lei,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ContactWorld基准，系统研究接触丰富操作中视觉-触觉世界模型的表示特性，发现空间结构化且时间连续的表示（如点云）能显著提升规划性能，而触觉的有效性取决于跨模态表示兼容性，且在长时域规划中愈发重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要世界模型从多模态感官观测中推理复杂接触动力学，但何种表示属性能够支持稳定长时域规划尚不明确。

Method: 构建ContactWorld基准，包含12项接触丰富操作任务；系统比较不同观测表示（腕部视角、前视角、点云、触觉力场等）在规划性能上的表现。

Result: 点云观测将平均规划成功率从20.7%/22.0%提升至32.1%；点云结合触觉力场表示进一步提升至36.1%；触觉在长时域规划中作用更显著。

Conclusion: 空间结构化且时间连续的表示是接触丰富操作中世界模型的关键，跨模态表示兼容性比单纯的模态扩充更重要，触觉在长时域中不可或缺。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires world models to reason over complex contact dynamics from multimodal sensory observations. However, it remains unclear which representation properties fundamentally support stable long-horizon planning in contact-rich settings. In this paper, we present ContactWorld, a benchmark and systematic empirical study of vision-tactile world models spanning 12 contact-rich manipulation tasks, including insertion, disassembly, screwing, and exploratory interaction. Across extensive experiments, we find that representations that are both spatially structured and temporally continuous consistently achieve the strongest planning performance. In particular, point-cloud observations improve average planning success rates from 20.7% with wrist-view observations and 22.0% with front-view observations to 32.1%. We further find that the effectiveness of tactile sensing depends critically on cross-modal representation compatibility rather than modality scaling alone. Combining point-cloud observations with tactile force-field representations, which preserve richer spatial structure and interaction dynamics, further improves performance to 36.1%, yielding the strongest overall planning performance across all evaluated tasks. Moreover, tactile sensing becomes increasingly important under long-horizon planning objectives, where compounding prediction errors and contact uncertainty accumulate over time. Together, these findings highlight the importance of representation structure, multimodal compatibility, and long-horizon robustness in vision-tactile world models for contact-rich robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [114] [Impedance MPC with Disturbance Estimation for Dexterous Hand Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14606)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种执行器无关的阻抗模型预测控制（Impedance MPC）框架，用于灵巧手指的精确轨迹跟踪与安全柔顺接触控制。该框架通过代数前馈将各类肌腱传动系统简化为常系数双积分器，结合离线预计算QP代价逆矩阵与500 Hz在线优化，在液压手指和LEAP Hand仿真中实现了相比传统阻抗控制数十至数千倍的精度提升，并严格保证了稳定性与接触力硬约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手需要在精确跟踪指尖轨迹与维持安全柔顺接触之间取得平衡，而固定增益控制器无法同时满足这两个相互冲突的目标。现有方法难以在统一框架下兼顾高精度跟踪、安全力约束以及对不同传动方式（液压、线缆、气动等）的通用性。

Method: 提出Impedance MPC框架，采用常数A_d零偏置架构（继承自pHRI），通过代数前馈将各类肌腱传动统一为常系数双积分器模型；QP代价逆矩阵可离线预计算，在线以500 Hz运行10步滚动时域二次规划，同时约束接触力（ISO/TS 15066标准）、执行器限幅和加加速度；采用仅需编码器的增广卡尔曼扰动状态估计器，实现任意恒定接触载荷下的零稳态误差。

Result: 在液压手指平台上（额外含压力与空化约束），500 Hz的卡尔曼MPC实现0.5 mrad RMS、0.1 mrad稳态误差、6.6 mrad峰值偏移（1.5 Nm接触力下），分别比传统阻抗控制好183倍、1500倍和23倍；首动刚度可达18→323 Nm/rad（随更新率变化）；在16自由度LEAP Hand MuJoCo仿真中，2.5 N抓取负载扰动下0.7秒内恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架在执行器无关、高精度力位混合控制方面展现出显著优势，不仅继承了pHRI架构的稳定性保证，还可扩展至多指灵巧手系统，为灵巧操作中的精确性与安全性平衡提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous hands must simultaneously track precise finger trajectories and maintain safe, compliant contact -- objectives in tension for any fixed-gain controller. We present an actuator-agnostic Impedance Model Predictive Control (Impedance MPC) framework for dexterous fingers, instantiating the constant-$A_d$ offset-free architecture established for physical human-robot interaction (pHRI); its stability, recursive-feasibility, and input-to-state-stability guarantees are inherited by preserving the architectural assumptions. An algebraic feedforward reduces the tendon transmission -- hydraulic, cable, pneumatic, twisted-string, or series-elastic -- to a constant-coefficient double integrator, so the QP cost inverse is precomputed offline and a 10-step receding-horizon quadratic program runs at 500\,Hz while enforcing hard constraints on contact force (ISO/TS 15066), actuation limits, and jerk. An encoder-only augmented-Kalman disturbance state drives steady-state error to zero under any constant contact load. On a hydraulically actuated finger -- the worked example platform, adding pressure and cavitation constraints -- the 500\,Hz Kalman MPC attains 0.5\,mrad RMS, 0.1\,mrad steady-state, and 6.6\,mrad peak deflection under 1.5\,Nm contact: 183$\times$, 1500$\times$, and 23$\times$ better than classical impedance. The realized first-move stiffness (18$\to$323\,Nm/rad with update rate) is independently verified. The architecture scales to a 16-DOF LEAP Hand MuJoCo simulation, recovering from 2.5\,N grasp-load disturbances within 0.7\,s.

</details>


### [115] [AnyGoal: Vision-Language Guided Multi-Agent Exploration for Training-Free Lifelong Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13878)
*MoniJesu James,Marcelino Julio Fernando,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyGoal是一种无需训练的多机器人架构，以VLM为核心进行前沿探索，通过共享的2D高斯贝叶斯价值图（BVM）协调智能体，在GOAT-Bench上实现了52.4%的子任务成功率，比Modular GOAT提升27.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端导航策略在跨分布场景中性能严重下降，模块化流水线受限于封闭集目标检测的召回率，3D快照记忆系统依赖密集的视图相关表示且维护成本高。

Method: 提出AnyGoal架构：1) VLM核心的前沿探索；2) 共享2D高斯贝叶斯价值图(BVM)实现终身证据累积；3) 深度锥掩码下的精度加权VLM分数融合；4) 前沿排序使用VLM软评分与贝叶斯UCB项的凸组合；5) 贪婪分配器含空间分离惩罚和承诺滞后机制。

Result: 双智能体系统在GOAT-Bench验证集上达到52.4%子任务成功率(SPL=12.7%)，超越Modular GOAT(24.9%)达27.5个百分点；单智能体AnyGoal达41.9%。四路感知消融表明开放词汇检测器将主导失败模式从探索转向目标验证。

Conclusion: AnyGoal通过决策架构而非感知改进实现性能提升，证明了VLM驱动的贝叶斯探索框架在跨分布目标导航中的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end navigation policies trained on large simulation corpora degrade sharply when transferred to out-of-distribution scenes, categories, or goal modalities. Modular pipelines such as Modular GOAT are bottlenecked by closed-set object detection recall, while 3D snapshot-memory systems (e.g. 3D-Mem) accumulate dense, view-dependent representations that are heavy to maintain. We present AnyGoal, a training-free multi-robot architecture that places a Vision-Language Model (VLM) at the core of frontier-based exploration and coordinates agents through a shared 2D Gaussian Bayesian Value Map (BVM). The BVM maintains a per-pixel (mu, sigma^2) posterior over goal relevance, updated via precision-weighted fusion of VLM scores through a depth-cone mask, and is never reset between subtasks, yielding lifelong evidence accumulation. Frontiers are ranked by a convex blend of a VLM-as-judge softmax and a Bayesian UCB term on the BVM. A greedy allocator with spatial-separation penalty and commitment hysteresis distributes frontiers across agents without a centralized controller. On the full GOAT-Bench val unseen split (360 episodes, 2,669 subtasks), our dual-agent system achieves 52.4% Subtask SR at 12.7% SPL--state of the art under the strict physical regime (discrete 0.25 m steps, no teleportation, 42 deg HFOV) and a +27.5 pp improvement over Modular GOAT (24.9%). Single-agent AnyGoal achieves 41.9% Subtask SR, showing gains arise from the decision architecture. A four-way perception ablation shows that open-vocabulary detectors shift the dominant failure mode from exploration to goal verification.

</details>


### [116] [Whole-Body Impedance Model Predictive Control for Safe Physical Human--Robot Interaction on Floating-Base Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14617)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种三层架构的阻抗MPC框架，将固定基座的双层阻抗MPC扩展到浮动基座机器人，通过质心MPC规划接触力、优先级WBC层平衡、以及卡尔曼增强的滚动时域QP预测和抑制人机交互扰动，实现零稳态误差和1kHz以上的实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮动基座机器人在刚性接触约束下需要平衡的同时与人安全交互，现有WBC框架要么将全关节空间分配给 locomotion，要么依赖固定增益阻抗反馈，在持续物理人机交互力下累积稳态误差。

Method: 三层架构：(1) 质心MPC在500ms时域内规划接触力；(2) 优先级WBC层通过接触一致零空间投影将平衡分解为关节扭矩；(3) 残差零空间由滚动时域QP控制，使用卡尔曼增强状态估计预测和抑制pHRI扰动。接触一致反馈线性化将末端执行器简化为双积分器，实现QP成本的离线预计算和1kHz以上运行。协方差膨胀协议保持接触模式切换时的扰动估计，保证零稳态误差。阻抗等价定理证明无穷时域极限恢复经典任务空间阻抗律。

Result: 在17自由度双足机器人和Unitree G1人形机器人上进行了仿真验证，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提三层架构有效解决了浮动基座机器人在物理人机交互下的平衡控制问题，通过接触一致反馈线性化和协方差膨胀协议实现了零稳态误差和高速实时控制。

Abstract: Floating-base robots must balance under rigid contact constraints while interacting safely with humans. Existing whole-body control~(WBC) frameworks allocate the full joint space to locomotion or rely on fixed-gain impedance feedback that accumulates steady-state error under sustained physical human--robot interaction~(pHRI) forces. This paper extends the authors' fixed-base two-layer Impedance MPC to floating-base platforms through a three-level architecture: a centroidal MPC plans contact forces over a 500\,ms horizon; a priority-driven WBC layer resolves balance into joint torques through contact-consistent null-space projection; and the residual null space is governed by a receding-horizon quadratic program~(QP) that predicts and rejects pHRI disturbances using a Kalman-augmented state. A contact-consistent feedback linearization reduces the arm end-effector plant to a double integrator with a \emph{constant} state matrix within each contact mode, enabling offline precomputation of the QP cost and ${\geq}1$\,kHz operation. A covariance-inflation protocol preserves the disturbance estimate across contact-mode switches, guaranteeing zero steady-state error under bounded constant pHRI loads, and an Impedance Equivalence Theorem shows the infinite-horizon limit recovers a classical task-space impedance law whose effective mass, damping, and stiffness adapt to posture and contact configuration. Simulations on a 17-DOF biped and the Unitree G1 humanoid validate the design.

</details>


### [117] [Guided Diffusion with Distilled Vision-Language Reliability for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13883)
*Ivan Valuev,Iana Zhura,Valerii Serpiva,Didar Seyidov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向3D无人机导航的可靠性感知扩散规划器，通过轻量级网络蒸馏视觉语言模型生成场景级可靠性热力图，并结合可微分的两阶段ESDF代价函数，在仿真和实物实验中显著降低障碍物碰撞率（40.3%→9.6%），提升路径可靠性（0.588→0.925）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自主无人机导航将感知、建图、规划分离为独立阶段，导致误差传播、延迟累积且需场景级重新调参；端到端生成模型虽消除接口，但无法识别不可靠观测（如玻璃、镜面、过曝光区域），将其当作有效证据用于规划。

Method: 1) 轻量级网络蒸馏视觉语言模型的开放词汇推理能力，实时生成场景级可靠性热力图；2) 扩散模型以观测+热力图作为条件生成轨迹；3) 可微分的两阶段ESDF代价将深度物理障碍与高不可靠区域虚拟障碍统一处理，无需重新训练即可泛化到未知环境。

Result: 仿真与实物四旋翼飞行器上，障碍物违规率从40.3%降至9.6%，路径平均可靠性从0.588提升至0.925；消融实验中去除可靠性项可靠性从0.898降至0.783；蒸馏使框架比完整VLM快2倍。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的扩散规划器可有效解决端到端模型对不可靠观测的鲁棒性问题，蒸馏策略在保持性能的同时满足实时性要求，是无人机安全导航的有效方案。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV navigation is conventionally solved by pipelines that separate perception, mapping, and planning into distinct stages, which propagates errors, accumulates latency, and requires environment-specific retuning. End-to-end generative models remove these interfaces by mapping raw observations directly to trajectories, but inherit a subtle failure mode: trained on clean data, they cannot recognise when an observation is unreliable, and treat degraded regions such as glass, mirrors, and overexposed surfaces as valid evidence for planning. We present a reliability-aware diffusion planner for 3D UAV navigation. It conditions trajectory generation on the observation together with a scene-level reliability heatmap that marks where perception cannot be trusted, produced by a lightweight network that distils the open-vocabulary reasoning of a vision-language model within the real-time planning budget. To generalise to unseen environments without retraining, we steer the denoising process with a differentiable two-stage ESDF cost that treats physical obstacles from depth and virtual obstacles from highly unreliable regions on equal footing. In simulation and on a real quadrotor, our planner produces markedly safer trajectories than a state-of-the-art diffusion baseline, reducing the obstacle-violation rate from 40.3% to 9.6% and raising the mean reliability of traversed regions from 0.588 to 0.925. Ablating the reliability term alone drops mean reliability from 0.898 to 0.783, confirming it as the decisive component, while distillation runs the framework up to 2 times faster than the full vision-language model.

</details>


### [118] [PhysVLA: Towards Physically-Grounded VLA for Embodied Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13886)
*Namai Chandra,Shriram Damodaran,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysVLA是一种即插即用的推理时框架，通过相位有限状态机和选择性欧拉-拉格朗日门对冻结的VLA骨干网络输出的动作进行物理一致性校正，无需重新训练或微调，在多个VLA模型和机器人平台上显著提升了任务成功率（最高+50%）、轨迹稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型仅拟合演示数据，缺乏物理原理（如刚体动力学、接触约束）的显式建模，导致标准时序平滑策略虽能改善轨迹质量但会引入短期记忆无法解决的失败模式，存在关键物理学鸿沟。

Method: PhysVLA框架包含两层校正：(i)相位有限状态机，将任务分解为接近、抓取、运输、放置等离散阶段；(ii)选择性欧拉-拉格朗日门，仅在动力学检测器发现运动动力学不一致时激活，对预测动作施加物理约束。

Result: 在LIBERO-Spatial和Franka Panda上，PhysVLA带来成功率提升最高17%、稳定性提升最高19%、轨迹效率提升最高15%，无任务回归现象；轨迹抖动鲁棒性提升10倍；在真实Agilex Piper手臂上成功率提升最高50%。

Conclusion: 物理感知可以作为可组合、与骨干网络无关的运行时模块，在不重新训练的前提下显著提升VLA模型在实际物理任务中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at mapping visual inputs and natural language instructions directly to robotic control policies. However, because they are trained primarily to fit behavioural demonstration data, they do not explicitly enforce fundamental physical principles such as rigid-body dynamics or contact constraints. This exposes a critical physics gap: standard temporal smoothing applied on top of single-step or chunked VLAs trades trajectory quality for added failures that short-term memory cannot resolve. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysVLA (Physics-VLA), a plug-and-play, inference-time framework designed to wrap any frozen VLA backbone without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, with less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. PhysVLA intercepts the predicted control action, captures only the simulator or system state, and applies a dual-layered correction: (i) a phase-aware finite-state machine that structures discrete task segments (approach, grasp, transport, and place), and (ii) a selective Euler-Lagrange gate that activates only when a dynamics oracle detects kinodynamic inconsistency. Evaluated across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on LIBERO-Spatial with a 7-DoF Franka Panda, the framework delivers absolute success rate increases of up to 17% and stability increases of up to 19% with no per-task regressions, improves trajectory efficiency by up to 15% across all four backbones, and shows up to a 10x improvement in trajectory jerk robustness on a Robosuite Lift cross-simulator sweep. We further validate the framework on a real Agilex Piper arm with a pick-and-place task, confirming that PhysVLA transfers to physical hardware without retraining, with success-rate improvements of up to 50%, establishing physical awareness as a composable, backbone-agnostic runtime module.

</details>


### [119] [An Attention-based Model for Robust Forecasting with Missing Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13970)
*Zhitian Zhang,Wenjie Zi,Yunduz Rakhmangulova,Saghar Irandoust,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件变分自编码器（CVAE）和Transformer架构的多模态模型，能够在训练和推理阶段处理缺失模态数据，并在人类轨迹预测和机器人操作预测两个任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人系统常面临传感器数据不完整的问题，而现有大多数多模态模型假设训练和推理时所有模态均可用，限制了其在机器人感知与决策中的实用性。

Method: 将模型构建为条件变分自编码器（CVAE），采用基于Transformer的注意力机制，学习统一且维度固定的表示，即使部分模态缺失也能有效训练和推理。

Result: 在人类轨迹预测和机器人操作预测两个任务的五个多模态数据集上，模型能够从缺失模态数据中有效学习，并优于先前的多模态融合方法。

Conclusion: 所提模型能够在缺失模态条件下进行训练和推理，学习鲁棒的多模态统一表示，适用于实际机器人系统中的不完整传感器数据场景。

Abstract: Learning with missing modalities is a fundamental challenge in multimodal robot learning, as real-world robotic systems often operate in environments with incomplete sensor data. Attention-based models are appealing for processing multimodal data because they can handle multiple modalities with a single backbone network. However, most multimodal models assume that all modalities are available during both training and inference, limiting their applicability in robotic perception and decision-making. In this paper, we introduce a multimodal model designed to handle missing modalities during both training and inference. The model is formulated as a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) and incorporates a transformer-based architecture that leverages attention mechanisms to learn a unified, fixed-dimensional representation, even when some modalities are missing.
