We systematize existing work, summarize key takeaways, highlight open research questions, and offer a perspective on pervasive industry misconceptions, concluding that AI and crypto are still in the very early stages of meaningful integration.

</details>


### [158] [Software Dark Matter: Gazing at Uncharted Files to Navigate SBOM Integrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13966)
*Abhishek Reddypalle,Dennis Roellke,Santiago Torres-Arias*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"软件暗物质"(SDM)概念，指代制品文件系统中未被元数据追踪的安全关键文件。通过实现DARKFILES工具分析DockerHub、Maven Central、插件市场和企业环境四个生态系统，发现SDM导致SBOM驱动的流水线存在系统性可见性缺口，并关联到已知CVE及敏感信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件供应链依赖以元数据为中心的SBOM范式，假设制品由包管理器声明定义，但这种假设存在根本性缺陷，导致安全可见性缺口。

Method: 提出SDM概念和通用保真度度量（未追踪文件数/总文件数），实现DARKFILES参考工具，在DockerHub、Maven Central、Jenkins插件、OpenVSX和企业环境四个异质生态系统中进行实证分析，交叉引用未追踪包与已知CVE数据库。

Result: 发现"打包滞后"现象（官方元数据多个版本滞后于实际内容）；SDM使SBOM驱动的流水线无法发现易受攻击的软件；直接发现三个确认的高严重度CVE；SDM与机密信息和加密密钥高度相关。

Conclusion: 元数据为中心的SBOM范式存在系统性漏洞，SDM是真实且严重的安全威胁，需要超越元数据的透明度机制来应对软件供应链安全挑战。

Abstract: Modern software supply chains have evolved into vast, heterogeneous networks where transparency - the granular understanding of all software components - is now a critical security requirement. While Software Bills of Materials (SBOMs) have emerged as the primary mechanism for this transparency, current industry practices rely on a metadata-centric paradigm that assumes an artifact is defined solely by its package manager declarations. We posit that this assumption is fundamentally flawed, creating a systemic visibility gap we define as Software Dark Matter (SDM). SDM represents the set of security-critical files present in an artifact's filesystem that are unaccounted for by its associated metadata. We implement a reference tool, DARKFILES, and use it to analyze four ecosystems of disjoint nature: DockerHub, Maven Central, plugin/extension marketplaces (Jenkins plugins and OpenVSX), and a real-world enterprise environment.
  Our research makes the following contributions: we introduce a general-purpose metric for artifact fidelity calculating SDM as the ratio of untracked files per total file count. We introduce Packaging Lag, a phenomenon where official metadata remains out-of-date across multiple versions before catching up to an artifact's actual content. We demonstrate that SDM exposes vulnerable software invisible to SBOM-driven pipelines both by cross-referencing untracked packages against known CVE databases and through the direct discovery of three confirmed high-severity CVEs, showing that SDM is highly correlated with sensitive information including secrets and cryptographic keys.

</details>


### [159] [Choric Masking in Ambient Release Systems: A Finite Certificate Calculus for Trace Indistinguishability under Bounded Audiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13967)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文为环境释放系统（ambient release systems）建立了一个有限的证书演算（finite certificate calculus），该系统是一种阶段性的概率环境，其中受保护的坐标不被直接观测，但可通过可见角色、时序、重复运动、有限注意力、关联房间及释放后状态保持统计可读性。安全概念“合唱遮蔽”（choric masking）要求受保护轨迹的迹分布位于或接近在特定受众可用测试下的可接受覆盖迹的凸包内。对于有限视界，迹分布形成多面体，受众诱导测量算子，遮蔽变为投影测量空间中的交集。暴露通过分离超平面、核障碍、假设检验界、Fano型定位下界及下游房间的支持分离来认证。该演算区分迹残差与载体定位、全迹暴露与注意力过滤暴露、首房间遮蔽与延迟释放后暴露、未解决系统压力与载体风险。它证明了精确与近似遮蔽的测量-多面体等价性、对偶分离证书、注意力透镜的数据处理定律、凝视稀疏观测的光圈恒等式、强制性唯一手势的下界、链接释放的合成规则，以及一个重复房间债务定理，展示了未解决压力如何扩大选择范围并将成本转移至覆盖群体而不定位源。结果是一个有限的、可检查的语言，用于审计公开释放系统中的隐私、不可链接性、侧信道泄漏和问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在环境释放系统中，受保护的敏感信息（如位置坐标）虽不被直接观测，但可通过各种侧信道（可见角色、时序、运动模式、注意力限制、房间关联等）被统计推断。现有隐私模型缺乏一个统一的、可检查的形式化框架来认证这类系统中的隐私泄露风险。本文旨在填补这一空白，为这类系统的隐私审计提供一个有限的证书演算。

Method: 作者提出了一种有限的证书演算，其核心方法包括：(1) 将安全概念形式化为“合唱遮蔽”，要求受保护轨迹的分布落在或接近覆盖轨迹分布的凸包内；(2) 利用有限视界下迹分布形成多面体、受众诱导测量算子的特性，将遮蔽问题转化为投影测量空间中的交集问题；(3) 通过分离超平面、核障碍、假设检验界、Fano型定位下界、支持分离等多种技术手段来认证暴露；(4) 证明测量-多面体等价性、对偶分离证书、数据处理定律、光圈恒等式等核心定理。

Result: 本文建立了一个完整的有限证书演算，能够区分多种暴露类型（迹残差vs载体定位、全迹vs注意力过滤暴露等），并证明了若干关键定理（测量-多面体等价性、对偶分离证书、数据处理定律、光圈恒等式、重复房间债务定理等），最终形成了一个可检查的有限语言用于审计隐私、不可链接性、侧信道泄漏和问责性。

Conclusion: 本文为环境释放系统的隐私审计提供了一个有限的、可检查的证书演算语言，能够系统性地认证各种类型的隐私泄露和侧信道风险，并解决了暴露类型区分、组成规则和成本转移等关键问题。该工作为敏感信息在公开释放系统中的隐私保护提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a finite certificate calculus for ambient release systems, staged probabilistic environments in which a protected coordinate is not observed directly but can remain statistically readable through visible roles, timing, repeated movement, bounded attention, linked rooms, and post-release state. The security notion, choric masking, requires the trace law induced by a protected locus to lie inside or near the convex hull of admissible cover traces under the tests available to a specified audience. For finite horizons, trace laws form polytopes, audiences induce measurement operators, and masking becomes intersection in the projected measurement space. Exposure is certified by separating hyperplanes, kernel obstructions, hypothesis-testing bounds, Fano-type localization lower bounds, and support separation in downstream rooms. The calculus distinguishes trace residue from carrier localization, full-trace exposure from attention-filtered exposure, first-room masking from delayed post-release exposure, and unresolved system pressure from carrier hazard. It proves measurement-polytope equivalence for exact and approximate masks, dual separation certificates, data-processing laws for attention lenses, aperture identities for gaze-thinned observation, lower bounds for mandatory unique gestures, composition rules for linked releases, and a repeated-room debt theorem showing how unresolved pressure can broaden selection and shift cost onto cover populations without localizing the source. The result is a finite, checkable language for auditing privacy, unlinkability, side-channel leakage, and accountability in public-facing release systems.

</details>


### [160] [Hidden in Plain Sight: Benchmarking Agent Safety Against Decomposition Attacks with DECOMPBENCH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13994)
*Vikhyath Kothamasu,Virginia Smith,Chhavi Yadav*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DeCompBench基准，专门用于评估LLM智能体在分解攻击下的安全性。实验表明，最先进智能体对整体有害任务拒绝率高，但对分解后的良性子任务拒绝率显著降低，且常无意中完成恶意目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准未明确捕捉分解攻击这种将有害任务拆解为独立无害子任务以绕过安全机制的新型威胁，且无法代表真实的对抗执行流程。

Method: 采用"分解即设计"原则，利用图形化框架将有害任务分解为各自无害但可执行的子任务，并构建真实的工作流。

Result: 最先进智能体对整体有害任务拒绝率高，但对分解变体拒绝率显著降低，且常无意中完成对抗目标。

Conclusion: 需要针对分解攻击的安全评估及相应的防御机制。

Abstract: LLM-based Agents are becoming increasingly capable and widely deployed, creating growing incentives for adversarial misuse in the real-world. A key emerging threat is Decomposition Attacks \cite{glukhov2024breach, jones2024adversaries} in which a harmful task is broken into simpler, benign subtasks that evade safety mechanisms when executed separately but cumulatively fulfill the malicious intent. Although recent benchmarks assess agent safety in multi-turn and multi-tool-use settings, they do not explicitly capture this form of decompositional misuse and may not represent realistic adversarial execution flows. To this end, we introduce DeCompBench, a benchmark designed specifically to evaluate agentic safety under decomposition attacks. DeCompBench is created with a decomposition-by-design principle using a graphical framework and enables harmful task decomposition into individually benign and executable subtasks with realistic workflows. Our experiments using a custom decomposer show that state-of-the-art agents exhibit high refusal rates on monolithic harmful tasks, but significantly lower refusal rates on their decomposed variants, while often inadvertently fulfilling the adversarial objectives. These findings underscore the need for safety evaluations against decomposition attacks and corresponding defenses. Our dataset is publicly available and can be found at https://huggingface.co/datasets/decompositionbench/DeCompBench.

</details>


### [161] [Defending the Core: A Centrality-Based Protection Strategy for Supply Chain Security in npm Dependency Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14036)
*Zixin Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对npm依赖网络（53,481个包和78,520条依赖边）的建模分析，发现其呈现无标度拓扑结构，易受针对高度枢纽节点的定向攻击。为此提出双重防御策略：中心性节点加固与依赖权重告警系统。模拟表明，对前1%节点实施严格安全协议并修剪30%的结构性无关边，可防止网络崩溃并阻断级联恶意软件感染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件供应链（如npm依赖网络）高度依赖共享开源依赖，虽然促进了快速开发，但也引入了系统性漏洞风险。作者旨在揭示npm依赖网络的结构脆弱性，并提出有效的防御策略。

Method: 对53,481个包和78,520条依赖边进行建模，将网络分类为无标度拓扑；提出双重防御策略：基于中心性的节点加固（Centrality-Based Node-Hardening）和依赖权重告警系统（Dependency Weight Warning）；通过多种攻击场景下的网络模拟进行验证。

Result: 对前1%的节点实施严格安全协议并结合修剪30%的结构性无关边，可防止灾难性网络崩溃并中和级联恶意软件感染。

Conclusion: 无标度拓扑的npm依赖网络对针对高中心性枢纽节点的定向攻击具有固有脆弱性，而所提出的双重防御策略能够以较小的代价有效提升供应链安全性。

Abstract: The modern software supply chain, taking Node Package Manager (npm) dependency network for example, relies heavily on shared open-source dependencies. While this promotes rapid development, it introduces systemic vulnerabilities as well. Concerning this potential risk, we analyze the npm dependency network by modeling 53,481 packages and 78,520 dependency edges, and classify the network as a scale-free topology. Thus, we demonstrate its inherent vulnerability to targeted attacks on high-degree hubs. To mitigate this, we propose and evaluate a dual-pronged defense strategy consisting of Centrality-Based Node-Hardening and Dependency Weight Warning system. Moreover, by simulating the network under various attack scenarios, we prove that applying strict security protocols to just the top 1% of nodes, combined with pruning 30% of structurally trivial edges, prevents catastrophic network collapse and neutralizes cascading malware infections. The source code can be found at https://github.com/5tarWhee1/Centrality-Based-Protection-Strategy-for-Supply-Chain-Security-in-npm-Dependency-Network.

</details>


### [162] [Hierarchical Identity-Based Signature with Designated Aggregator from Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14090)
*Stuti Kumari,Kunal Dey,Vikas Srivastava,Sumit Kumar Debnath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于格密码的分层聚合签名方案（HIBS-DA），允许不同层级用户生成可聚合的紧凑签名，以降低通信和验证成本，适用于大学、企业等大规模组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级组织中多用户数据认证复杂且资源消耗大，现有方案效率不足，需要更高效的签名聚合机制。

Method: 提出HIBS-DA框架，并基于格密码（lattice-based cryptography）构造首个HIBS-DA方案，支持不同层级用户生成可聚合为单一紧凑签名的个体签名。

Result: 方案在安全性、正确性和抗伪造性方面均满足要求，显著降低了通信和验证开销。

Conclusion: 该方案适用于大学、企业、政府机构等大规模层级化组织环境，提供高效且安全的数据认证解决方案。

Abstract: In hierarchical organizations, authenticating data from multiple users can be complex and resource-intensive. Hierarchical Identity-Based Signature with Designated Aggregator (HIBS-DA) provides an efficient solution by allowing users at different levels to generate signatures that can be combined into a single, compact signature. We first introduce the HIBS-DA framework and present the {\em{first}} lattice-based construction of HIBS-DA. Our scheme allows users at different hierarchical levels to generate individual signatures that can be aggregated into a single, compact signature, reducing communication and verification costs. The proposed construction is secure, correct, and resistant to forgery, making it suitable for large-scale environments such as universities, corporations, and government agencies.

</details>


### [163] [SkillMutator: Benchmarking and Defending Language-and-Code Cross-modal Attacks on LLM Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14154)
*Youngduk Kim,Minkyoo Song,Seungwon Shin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMutator基准测试，用于检测LLM Agent技能中的语言-代码跨模态攻击。现有检测器仅能发现2%-17%的攻击。通过四阶段推理轨迹蒸馏框架，将前沿模型能力蒸馏至7B开源模型，检测率从17.1%提升至88.2%，超越GPT-4o-mini并达到前沿GPT-5.4水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在执行时通过加载Agent Skills扩展能力，但技能的安全评估需要跨模态推理（自然语言指令+可执行代码），形成了新的语言-代码攻击面。攻击者可在SKILL.md中呈现良性工作流的同时嵌入隐式指令，引导Agent窃取敏感文件。现有工作仅将技能视为提示注入向量或静态代码产物，跨模态交互产生的攻击未被充分研究。

Method: 提出SkillMutator基准，模拟13种攻击类别的对抗性变异过程，利用扫描器反馈迭代优化恶意技能。同时提出四阶段推理轨迹蒸馏框架，将前沿教师模型的推理轨迹蒸馏至小规模开放权重模型，实现本地化部署的检测器。

Result: 在最强SkillMutator子集上，开源检测器仅检测2%-8%，商业检测器仅9%-17%。蒸馏后的Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct检测率从17.1%提升至88.2%，超越GPT-4o-mini（23.7%）和GPT-5.4-mini（79.0%），达到GPT-5.4（86.8%）水平。

Conclusion: 不依赖昂贵前沿模型的情况下，对跨模态攻击进行实际防御是可行的。通过推理轨迹蒸馏，可在本地部署高检测率的扫描器，避免第三方数据暴露和高额API成本。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly extend their capabilities at runtime by loading Agent Skills, which pair natural-language specifications (SKILL.md) with executable scripts and resources. Because a skill's behavior relies on both natural-language instructions and executable code, assessing its safety requires cross-modal reasoning, creating a new language-and-code attack surface. Attackers can present a benign workflow in SKILL.md while embedding implicit directives that steer the agent to exfiltrate sensitive files, even if the scripts appear harmless. This attack surface remains understudied; prior work treats skills merely as prompt-injection vectors or static code artifacts, leaving attacks emerging from cross-modal interactions largely unmeasured. In our evaluation, open-source and commercial skill scanners detect only 2%-8% and 9%-17% of such attacks, respectively. To address this gap, we introduce SkillMutator, the first benchmark for install-time detection of language-and-code cross-modal attacks on Agent Skills. It emulates an adversarial mutation process across 13 attack categories, iteratively refining malicious skills using scanner feedback to make injected behaviors indistinguishable from legitimate workflows. We further propose a four-phase reasoning-trajectory distillation framework to distill frontier-teacher traces into smaller open-weight models. This produces a locally deployable scanner avoiding third-party data exposure and excessive API costs. On the strongest SkillMutator subset (n=76), our distilled model (Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct) improves detection from 17.1% to 88.2%, surpassing GPT-4o-mini (23.7%) and GPT-5.4-mini (79.0%), and reaching frontier-level GPT-5.4 (86.8%). These results show practical defense against cross-modal attacks is feasible without relying on costly frontier models.

</details>


### [164] [Security Evaluation of Mobile Banking Applications in Sudan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14165)
*Abdelmonim Naway*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对苏丹四款主流移动银行应用（Bankak、Fawry、Okash、Sahil，覆盖160万用户）进行了安全审计。使用MobSF和Quixxi进行SAST分析，依据OWASP MASVS评估，结果映射到CWE。发现Bankak风险最高（12个漏洞），包括缺乏SSL证书绑定和不安全的TrustManager实现，易受中间人攻击。所有应用普遍存在安全随机数生成缺陷（CWE-330），部分使用已弃用加密算法，且所有应用保留调试符号（100%），降低了逆向工程门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 苏丹金融行业快速数字化导致移动银行应用激增，但安全审计未能跟上步伐，现有研究缺乏对该国金融科技生态系统中应用安全状况的系统性评估，存在关键研究空白。

Method: 利用Mobile Security Framework (MobSF)和Quixxi进行静态应用安全测试（SAST）；依据OWASP移动应用安全验证标准（MASVS）进行评估；将发现的漏洞映射到通用弱点枚举（CWE）标识符；对四款应用的安全态势进行对比分析。

Result: Bankak风险最高（12个漏洞），缺乏SSL证书绑定和存在不安全的TrustManager实现；Fawry相对成熟（7个漏洞）；所有应用均存在安全随机数生成缺陷（CWE-330）；Bankak和Okash使用已弃用加密算法（MD5/SHA-1）；所有应用成功禁用ADB备份，但100%在发布APK中保留详细调试符号。

Conclusion: 研究填补了苏丹金融科技生态系统安全评估的空白，为开发者提供了可操作的技术建议，并提出了在行业中实施"安全设计"原则的战略路线图。

Abstract: The rapid digitalization of the Sudanese financial sector has precipitated a surge in Mobile Banking Applications (MBAs); however, this growth has frequently outpaced rigorous security auditing. This study provides a comprehensive technical audit of the four most widely used Sudanese MBAs( Bankak, Fawry, Okash, and Sahil )collectively serving a user base of over 1.6 million. Utilizing Static Application Security Testing (SAST) via the Mobile Security Framework (MobSF) and Quixxi, the applications were evaluated against the OWASP Mobile Application Security Verification Standard (MASVS). Findings were mapped to Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) identifiers to identify systemic vulnerabilities. Analysis revealed critical disparities in security posture. Bankak, the market leader, exhibited the highest risk profile (12 vulnerabilities), including a critical absence of SSL certificate pinning and unsafe TrustManager implementations, rendering it highly susceptible to Man-in-the-Middle (MitM) attacks. While Fawry demonstrated relative maturity (7 vulnerabilities), a universal failure was observed across all four applications regarding secure random number generation (CWE-330), potentially compromising session token integrity. Additionally, Bankak and Okash were found to utilize deprecated cryptographic algorithms (MD5/SHA-1). Notably, all applications successfully disabled ADB backups, yet 100% retained verbose debugging symbols in production APKs, significantly lowering the barrier for reverse engineering. This research addresses a critical gap in the national fintech ecosystem by providing actionable technical recommendations for developers and a strategic roadmap for implementing "security-by-design" principles across the sector.

</details>


### [165] [From Prompts to Responses: Dual-Sided Data Leakage and Defense in Split Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14210)
*Zixuan Gu,Xiaojun Ye,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示Split-LLM中的双向隐私泄露风险（输入提示和输出响应），提出PIDI攻击方法和ADMI防御机制，实验证明ADMI能有效防御多种攻击且对任务性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注分割学习中通过中间表示反演攻击泄露私有输入提示，而忽略生成式输出响应也可能泄露敏感信息。本文旨在填补这一空白，同时应对双向隐私威胁。

Method: 提出两阶段攻击PIDI（补丁模型反演+双端初始化）同时攻击输入提示和输出响应；提出防御机制ADMI（适配器本地预热+互信息正则化）提供隐私保护。

Result: PIDI在长序列反演任务中显著优于先前方法；ADMI能有效防御PIDI及其他最先进反演攻击，同时最小化对任务性能的影响。

Conclusion: Split-LLM存在双向隐私泄露风险，ADMI可作为有效的防御方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in privacy-sensitive domains, where users must balance the risk of data exposure through external APIs against the high computational cost of local deployment. Split learning has therefore emerged as a promising paradigm for LLM fine-tuning and inference under limited local resources. However, it introduces new privacy risks. Prior work primarily studies leakage of private input prompts, typically via inversion attacks on intermediate representations, while the potential for sensitive information leakage through generative response outputs remains largely unexplored.
  In this work, we unveil novel vulnerabilities of Split-LLM by presenting Patched Model Inversion with Dual-Sided Initialization (PIDI), a two-stage attack that simultaneously targets both private input prompts and output responses in Split-LLM settings. It combines dual-sided initialization with a patched inversion strategy to tackle long sequences, substantially outperforming prior inversion methods. To counter threats from both sides, we further propose the Adapter-based DualGuard with Mutual Information Defense (ADMI), which integrates an adapter-based local warmup strategy and mutual information regularization to provide a strong empirical privacy protection with minimal impact on task performance. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks and models demonstrate that ADMI effectively defends against PIDI and other state-of-the-art inversion attacks. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/FLAIR-THU/VFLAIR-LLM.

</details>


### [166] [Security in a Workflow: Exploring Role-Based Agentic Architectures for Vulnerability Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14261)
*Srijita Basu,Miroslaw Staron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于角色的智能体工作流（Planner、Analyzer、Fixer、Verifier）用于软件漏洞分析与修复，在25个真实C/C++漏洞上评估，检测准确率44%，修复准确率19%，并探索了集成CodeQL静态分析工具的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM安全方法通常聚焦于检测或补丁生成等孤立任务，缺乏反映工业实践的多阶段智能体架构，存在研究与实际安全工程之间的差距。

Method: 构建了包含Planner、Analyzer、Fixer和Verifier四个角色的多智能体工作流，其中一个变体将Analyzer与CodeQL静态分析工具集成，使用nemotron-cascade-2:30b、qwen3-coder-next和gpt-oss:120b模型，在25个真实C/C++漏洞上进行评估。

Result: 漏洞检测准确率44%，修复准确率19%。

Conclusion: 该角色型智能体工作流在漏洞检测方面达到了与GPT 5.5相当的水平，但修复准确率仍较低，研究结果为软件安全从业者提供了实践启示。

Abstract: Secure software engineering in practice is a multi-stage workflow involving vulnerability analysis, remediation, and fix verification. However, current LLM-based software security approaches often focus on isolated tasks such as detection or patch generation, with limited attention to agentic architectures reflecting industrial workflow. This creates a gap between existing LLM-based vulnerability-handling methods and real-world practices. In this paper, we study a role-based agentic workflow for vulnerability analysis and mitigation consisting of Planner, Analyzer, Fixer, and Verifier roles. To explore the effect of static analysis tool, the analyzer agent was integrated with the CodeQL in one of the workflows. The models used include nemotron-cascade-2:30b, qwen3-coder-next, and gpt-oss:120b. Our evaluation uses 25 real-world C/C++ vulnerabilities. The study reports 44% vulnerability detection accuracy comparable to GPT 5.5 and 19% fix accuracy. We also list implications from this study in context of software security practitioners.

</details>


### [167] [AgentCyberRange: Benchmarking Frontier AI Systems in Realistic Cyber Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14295)
*Fengyu Liu,Jiarun Dai,Yihe Fan,Wuyuao Mai,Ziao Li,Bofei Chen,Jie Zhang,Zheng Lou,Bocheng Xiang,Qiyi Zhang,Xudong Pan,Geng Hong,Yuan Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgentCyberRange，首个开放、多靶场的自主网络攻击能力评估基础设施，包含110个漏洞、15个真实Web应用、8个企业级靶场（156个内部主机）及Cage工具链，覆盖Web利用和后渗透两个核心阶段。评估显示GPT-5.5+Codex表现最佳（Web利用16.1%，后渗透31.7%），并发现了未知漏洞和载荷变异等越界发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开基准仅评估孤立的网络安全技能（如CTF、漏洞复现），缺乏模拟真实入侵工作流（服务发现、立足、信息收集、横向移动）的开放性、可复现的多主机靶场，导致难以在现实攻击条件下早期观测前沿AI系统的新兴风险。

Method: 构建AgentCyberRange基准：集成110个跨15个真实Web应用和8个企业级靶场（156个内部主机）的漏洞，开发Cage工具链实现执行、编排、结果收集与验证。基准设计两个核心阶段——Web利用（探测暴露应用并验证漏洞）和后渗透（从初始立足点扩展至内部网络）。在匹配提示词和预算条件下评估6个前沿AI系统。

Result: GPT-5.5+Codex在Web利用任务中完成16.1%，在后渗透任务中完成31.7%；提供更具体提示后分别提升至33.0%和46.3%。此外发现流行项目中的未知漏洞以及绕过主机防御的载荷变异。

Conclusion: 开放靶场评估对于在真实且可复现条件下观测前沿AI系统的进攻性能力是必要的，研究结果揭示了当前AI系统的自主攻击能力现状及潜在风险。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems are increasingly capable of cybersecurity tasks, including codebase inspection, vulnerability detection, and exploitation. However, evaluating their offensive capabilities remains constrained by limited access to open, reproducible, multi-host cyber ranges. Existing public benchmarks capture isolated skills such as CTF solving, vulnerability reproduction, and exploit generation, but often abstract away realistic intrusion workflows: discovering exposed services, gaining a foothold, collecting internal information, and expanding compromise across hosts. This gap makes it difficult to observe emerging risks early, because frontier AI systems are rarely evaluated under realistic attack conditions.
  We introduce AgentCyberRange, the first open, multi-range infrastructure for measuring autonomous cyber attack capability in realistic cyber ranges. It combines 110 vulnerabilities across 15 real web applications and 8 enterprise-like cyber ranges with 156 internal hosts, plus Cage, a toolchain for execution, orchestration, result collection, and verification. The benchmark covers two core stages: web exploitation, where agents explore exposed applications and validate vulnerabilities, and post exploitation, where agents turn an initial foothold into broader internal compromise. We evaluate six frontier AI systems under matched prompts and budgets. GPT-5.5 with Codex performs best, solving 16.1% of web exploitation tasks and 31.7% of post-exploitation tasks; with more concrete hints, these rates increase to 33.0% and 46.3%. We also observe out-of-benchmark findings, including unknown vulnerabilities in popular projects, and payload mutation that bypasses host defenses. These results show that open cyber-range evaluation is necessary for observing emerging offensive capabilities under realistic and reproducible conditions.

</details>


### [168] [REPOSE: Quantifying the Price of Security in Weakly-Hard Real-Time Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14395)
*Vijay Banerjee,Monowar Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: REPOSE是一个运行时分析框架，用于评估弱实时控制系统的安全可行性，通过主动和被动结合的安全操作方式，在80%利用率下仅产生0.06%的开销，远优于硬实时系统29%的开销，有效实现了安全与安全的可量化权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IoT边缘设备的安全机制要么是机会式执行（可能因高利用率而完全不执行安全任务），要么是给每个任务都添加安全机制（导致性能严重下降），形成了"全有或全无"的困境，缺乏灵活的安全-安全权衡机制。

Method: 提出REPOSE分析框架，针对弱实时控制系统，基于任务当前行为分别采用主动和被动两种方式进行安全操作，而非在设计时施加悲观的额外开销。

Result: 在80%利用率下，REPOSE的可行性开销仅为0.06%，而硬约束系统开销为29%。通过经典控制系统的案例研究，验证了该框架能够有效分析和计算安全-安全权衡。

Conclusion: REPOSE为实时控制系统的运行时安全评估提供了一个鲁棒的框架，支持安全与安全的"有界权衡"，避免了传统方法的极端开销问题。

Abstract: In contemporary IoT edge devices with real-time requirements, security is primarily enforced through design-time parameters associated with security tasks, leading to mechanisms that operate in an \emph{opportunistic} manner. As a result, security checks are often performed as secondary operations. This approach can result in systems where no security tasks are executed due to high utilization by other tasks. An alternative approach taken in prior work is to add security mechanisms to every task in the system, resulting in substantially lower performance than that of a system with no security. These approaches have resulted in an \emph{all-or-nothing} scenario for edge device security, motivating numerous studies on the safety-security trade-off in real-time cyber-physical systems (RT-CPS). This study introduces an analytical framework -- REPOSE -- for evaluating the security feasibility of real-time control systems at runtime. REPOSE is developed for \textit{weakly-hard} real-time control systems that facilitate a ``bounded trade-off'' between safety and security. In contrast to imposing additional (pessimistic) design-time overhead as considered in some real-time security literature, REPOSE performs security operations in both \textit{proactive} and \textit{reactive} manners based on the task's current behavior. Our evaluations show that REPOSE can effectively add security operations to RT-CPS with a feasibility overhead of $0.06\%$ at $80\%$ utilization, compared to a $ 29\%$ overhead observed in systems with hard constraints. Through a case study of a classic control system, we also demonstrate that REPOSE provides a robust framework to \textit{analyze and calculate} the safety-security tradeoff.

</details>


### [169] [Breaking TinyML: Why Quantized Neural Networks Need Domain-Specific Security Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14427)
*Jacob Huckelberry,Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Yuke Zhang,Peter A. Beerel,James Mickens,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对TinyML硬件中量化神经网络（QNN）的安全漏洞，提出了一种两步攻击流水线，超越了现有最先进方法，揭示了硬件感知安全研究的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TinyML硬件加速器主要关注量化神经网络以降低功耗和尺寸，但量化在TinyML硬件中的安全问题尚未被充分研究，且缺乏专门针对TinyML硬件的攻击策略。

Method: 提出一种两步攻击流水线，专门针对TinyML硬件中的QNN实现进行攻击。

Result: 该两步攻击方法在QNN上下文中超越了当前最先进的攻击效果。

Conclusion: 研究表明需要更多硬件感知的安全性研究，以应对TinyML硬件面临的特有威胁。

Abstract: Most TinyML hardware accelerators focus on supporting Quantized Neural Networks (QNNs) to meet stringent constraints on power consumption and size. Despite this, the security aspects of quantization within TinyML hardware remain largely unexplored. Although previous studies indicate that QNNs demonstrate similar or enhanced robustness when compared to full-precision Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) against typical evasion attacks, no attack strategies tailored specifically for TinyML hardware have been proposed yet. This paper addresses this shortfall by demonstrating how a two-step attack pipeline can surpass the current state-of-the-art in the QNN context and shows the need for more hardware-aware security research.

</details>


### [170] [Securing the Future of IoMT in the Post-Quantum Era: An Edge-Native Federated Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14515)
*Taym Alshoghri,Deemah H. Tashman,Mohammad Reza Gerami,Soumaya Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对物联网医疗设备资源受限且数据高度敏感的特点，提出了一种集成后量子密码学（PQC）的Kubernetes框架，用于联邦学习环境中的IoMT系统。通过在树莓派测试床上的验证，分布式密码处理相比顺序设计显著降低了延迟，同时保持可行资源开销，为量子时代的医疗物联网安全通信提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoMT设备资源受限且处理高度敏感健康数据，联邦学习中的模型更新可能泄露医疗隐私，而新兴量子计算威胁着传统轻量级密码机制的长期安全性，因此亟需将后量子密码学集成到IoMT系统中。

Method: 提出了一种基于Kubernetes的可扩展框架，集成后量子密钥建立、轻量级加密和边缘原生编排技术，在树莓派测试床上进行验证，对比分布式与顺序密码处理方案的性能。

Result: 分布式密码处理显著降低了延迟，同时保持了可行的资源开销。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于为FL-enabled IoMT系统设计并验证了一种安全编排与通信框架，未来方向包括能量感知架构、智能安全优化及弹性下一代智能医疗物联网生态系统。

Abstract: Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) devices operate under strict resource constraints while handling highly sensitive health data, making security and privacy critical concerns. Federated learning (FL) further complicates this landscape, as model updates exchanged during training may unintentionally expose private medical information. Emerging quantum computing capabilities threaten the long-term viability of conventional lightweight cryptographic mechanisms, motivating the integration of Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) into IoMT systems. This article discusses key enabling technologies for quantum-resilient IoMT, including post-quantum key establishment, lightweight encryption, and edge-native orchestration. We propose a scalable Kubernetes-based framework that integrates PQC into FL-enabled IoMT environments and validate it on a Raspberry Pi testbed. Results demonstrate that distributed cryptographic processing significantly reduces latency compared to sequential designs while maintaining feasible resource overhead. The primary contribution of this work lies in the design and validation of a secure orchestration and communication framework for FL-enabled IoMT systems. We conclude by outlining future directions toward energy-aware architectures, intelligent security optimization, and resilient next-generation Intelligent Internet of Medical Things (IIoMT) ecosystems.

</details>


### [171] [From Shield to Target: Denial-of-Service Attacks on LLM-Based Agent Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14517)
*Yuguang Zhou,Xunguang Wang,Pingchuan Ma,Zhantong Xue,Zhaoyu Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示基于LLM的护栏在面对对抗性提示时存在拒绝服务(DoS)漏洞：攻击者通过精心构造的数据诱导护栏陷入长时间推理循环，导致系统瘫痪。作者提出了两种攻击框架（波束搜索优化和机制感知结构变异），实验表明该攻击可跨多种护栏架构、模型骨干（13-63倍令牌放大）和真实Agent系统（高达148倍延迟放大）生效，单文档即可饱和共享护栏基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based guardrails虽然能有效防御提示注入和越狱攻击，但其强大的推理和任务遵循能力本身带来了新漏洞：攻击者可注入精心构造的数据，使护栏陷入扩展推理循环，造成系统性的拒绝服务攻击。当前缺乏对这一可用性缺陷的系统性研究。

Method: 1) 波束搜索优化框架：利用LLM提议器配合策略库，自动生成最大化护栏推理长度的自然语言载荷；2) 机制感知结构变异攻击：利用护栏的schema遵循特性，通过结构性变异降低计算开销。实验包括独立评估（跨护栏架构、安全模板、Agent基准测试）和端到端真实Agent部署评估（web、桌面、代码、多Agent系统）。

Result: 在独立评估中，攻击可泛化至多种护栏架构和安全模板，载荷在单一开源代理上优化后可迁移至8种主流模型骨干（Claude、GPT、Gemini、DeepSeek、Qwen等），实现13-63倍令牌放大。在端到端真实Agent部署中，延迟放大可达148倍。单个中毒文档可饱和共享护栏基础设施，使共存Agent饿死并瘫痪整个系统。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了LLM护栏存在的可用性缺陷，强调了开发成本受限、推理鲁棒性强的护栏的迫切需求。

Abstract: LLM-based guardrails have emerged as a highly effective defense against prompt injection and jailbreak attacks in autonomous agents. However, we reveal that the very reasoning and task-following capabilities enabling this protection introduce a novel vulnerability: attackers can inject crafted data to trap the guardrail in extended reasoning loops, effectuating a systematic denial-of-service (DoS) attack. To systematically expose this threat, we design a beam-search optimization framework that crafts natural-language payloads to maximize guardrail reasoning length, utilizing an LLM proposer guided by a strategy bank. Based on the observation of guardrail's schema-following nature, we also provide another attack framework driven by mechanism-aware structural mutations with less computational load. The attack efficacy is systematically evaluated in two parts. First, in standalone evaluations, the attack generalizes across diverse guardrail architectures, safety templates, and agent benchmarks. Payloads optimized on a single open-source surrogate successfully transfer to eight leading model backbones (e.g., Claude, GPT, Gemini, DeepSeek, and Qwen), achieving a 13--63$\times$ token amplification. Second, in end-to-end real-world agent deployments (web, desktop, code, and multi-agent systems), the attack reveals up to a 148$\times$ latency amplification. We show that a single poisoned document can saturate shared guardrail infrastructures, effectively starving co-located agents and paralyzing the entire system. By uncovering this availability flaw, our work underscores the urgent need to develop cost-bounded, reasoning-robust guardrails.

</details>


### [172] [Detecting Bot Detection: Prevalence, Techniques, and Implications for Web Measurement Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14525)
*Ralf Gundelach,Michael Mühlhauser,Dominik Herrmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对10,000个网站、四种浏览器配置（共40K次页面访问）的测量研究发现，83%的顶会论文未讨论机器人检测导致的样本丢失问题。Chromium无头模式面临15%的软拦截率，82%的拦截归因于机器人检测，主要由Cloudflare（37%）和Akamai（26%）等集成检测的提供商造成。头部伪造实验表明75%的Chromium无头拦截由头部级信号引起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅测量机器人检测的部署情况，而忽略了检测导致的样本丢失（blocking-induced sample loss）对网络测量有效性的影响，且大多数论文未讨论这一问题。

Method: 对10,000个网站使用四种浏览器配置（40K次页面访问）进行测量研究，通过自定义工具检测网站是否探测自动化行为，建立机器人检测技术分类法，并进行头部伪造实验。

Result: Chromium无头模式软拦截率15%（其他配置7%），82%拦截由机器人检测引起（59%供应商确认，23%条件依赖性推断），Cloudflare和Akamai为主要拦截提供商。75%的Chromium无头拦截由头部级信号引起。

Conclusion: 机器人检测造成了系统性的、与提供商相关的样本丢失，而网络测量社区既未测量也未报告这一问题。对具体测量结果的后续影响有待未来研究。

Abstract: Browser automation frameworks are essential tools for security and privacy research on the web, yet bot detection scripts increasingly probe their artifacts, threatening measurement validity as automated browsers may be blocked or served different content. Prior work measures detection deployment, while we measure blocking-induced sample loss. Through a literature survey of top-tier security, privacy, and web measurement venues, we find that 83% of papers omit any discussion of bot detection blocking. To address this gap, we conduct a measurement study of 10,000 websites across four browser configurations (40K page visits in total) to quantify detection prevalence and employed techniques. Using custom instrumentation to detect when sites probe for automation, we develop a taxonomy of bot detection techniques and measure how often they appear in practice. Chromium headless encounters a 15% soft block rate compared to 7% for other configurations. Across all conditions, 82% of blocks are attributable to bot detection (59% vendor-confirmed, 23% inferred from condition-dependent blocking), predominantly by providers with integrated bot detection such as Cloudflare (37% block rate) and Akamai (26%). A header spoofing experiment establishes that 75% of Chromium-headless-only blocks are caused by header-level signals alone, yet JavaScript-based environment probing is more extensive than current blocking rates suggest. These findings demonstrate that bot detection creates systematic, provider-correlated sample loss that the web measurement community neither measures nor reports. The downstream effect on specific measurement outcomes remains future work.

</details>


### [173] [When Good Verifiers Go Bad: Self-Improving VLMs Can Regress on New Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14629)
*Jianzhe Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了验证器驱动的自我DPO训练中存在一个关键陷阱：验证器质量具有高度的任务特异性，强验证器在某个任务上有效，但在另一任务上可能低于阈值并导致学生模型性能显著倒退（3.4-10.9个百分点），即使DPO训练损失持续下降。这种失败模式具有置信度反转特性：更准确但依然错误的验证器反而造成更大损害。论文给出了基于进度门控回放的方差定理的机制解释，并提出了可操作部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证器驱动的自我DPO是自改进视觉语言模型的常用方法，其部署假设是更强的验证器产生更强的学生模型。本文旨在检验这一单调性假设是否始终成立，并揭示其在任务特异性验证器质量下可能失败的情况。

Method: 使用四个开源验证器组成能力阶梯，在MathVista、MMMU和BLINK三个基准上测试验证器质量的任务特异性。以Qwen-3-VL-2B和Qwen-2.5-VL-3B为学生模型，执行验证器驱动的自我DPO训练，测量验证器阈值效应、学生性能变化及损失函数行为，并通过方差定理给出机制解释。

Result: 验证器在MathVista上可提升学生性能，但在MMMU上任务准确率降至8%-23%，低于阈值，所有验证器均导致学生性能倒退3.4-10.9个百分点，DPO训练损失持续下降但性能恶化。失败模式下，更准确但依然错误的验证器比近乎随机的验证器造成更大损害。

Conclusion: 验证器驱动的自我DPO并非单调有效，验证器质量高度任务特异。部署前应测量目标任务的验证器准确率，按任务质量而非参数量排序验证器，并在阈值以上收益递减时设置验证器计算预算上限。

Abstract: Verifier-driven self-DPO is a common recipe for self-improving production visual-language models. In this setup, a frozen verifier scores candidate generations, the top- and bottom-scoring candidates form a preference example, and DPO updates the learner. The deployment-time assumption is monotone: a stronger verifier should yield a stronger student. We show that this assumption can fail because verifier quality is highly task-specific. On a four-rung open-source verifier ladder across MathVista, MMMU, and BLINK, the same verifiers that are above-threshold and improve a Qwen-3-VL-2B student on MathVista become sub-threshold on MMMU, where their task-rubric accuracy drops to 8% to 23%.
  In this regime, every verifier we tested silently regresses the student, producing drops of 3.4 to 10.9 percentage points below the frozen baseline while the DPO training loss continues to decrease. The regression replicates on a second student, Qwen-2.5-VL-3B. Moreover, within the failure regime, damage is confidence-inverted: the more accurate-but-still-wrong verifier causes larger regression than a near-random verifier, suggesting that progress-gated replay amplifies confidently wrong preference pairs. We give a compact mechanistic explanation via a variance theorem for progress-gated replay and its direction-mismatch failure mode.
  The deployment message is operational rather than purely diagnostic: before running any verifier-driven loop, teams should measure target-task rubric accuracy, rank verifiers by target-task rubric quality rather than parameter count, and treat diminishing returns in above-threshold regimes as a verifier-side compute budget cap.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [An Agentic Retrieval Framework for Autonomous Context-Aware Data Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13692)
*Hadi Fadlallah,Ibrahim Dhaini,Fatima Mubarak,Rima Kilany*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的智能体-检索框架，用于自主上下文感知的数据质量评估。该框架通过多智能体工作流解释自然语言描述的数据使用意图，生成可执行验证逻辑，并通过可行性验证阶段确保可靠性，最终实现可重复、可审计的自动化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据质量评估方法依赖静态规则或人工策略，缺乏对多样化使用场景的适应性，难以大规模自动化。大语言模型提供了新机遇，但存在可靠性、可落地性和执行安全性问题。

Method: 提出统一智能体-检索框架：1) 通过自然语言理解数据使用意图；2) 多智能体工作流推导上下文感知的评估策略；3) 生成可执行验证逻辑；4) 引入可行性验证阶段评估规范的真实性与可执行性，支持迭代优化；5) 确定性执行已验证逻辑以确保可复现和可审计。

Result: 原型系统在多个使用场景下的评估结果表明：评估结果能够根据不同使用意图自适应调整；可行性门控执行有效减少了不切实际或不可执行的规则生成。

Conclusion: 该方法为在现代数据驱动环境中部署自主可控的数据质量评估提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Data quality assessment is a critical prerequisite for effective data analytics and data-driven decision-making, yet it remains a challenging task due to the inherently context-dependent nature of data quality. Existing approaches often rely on static rules or manual assessment strategies, limiting their adaptability to diverse usage scenarios and constraining automation at scale. Recent advances in artificial intelligence, particularly large language models, offer new opportunities for automating data quality assessment, but raise concerns related to reliability, grounding, and execution safety.
  In this paper, we propose a unified agentic-retrieval framework for autonomous context-aware data quality assessment. The framework interprets natural-language descriptions of intended data usage, derives context-aware assessment strategies, and generates executable validation logic through a multi-agent workflow. To ensure operational reliability, the framework introduces a feasibility validation stage that evaluates the realism and executability of generated assessment specifications before execution, enabling iterative refinement when necessary. Accepted validation logic is executed deterministically to guarantee reproducible and auditable results.
  We implement the proposed framework as an end-to-end prototype and evaluate it across multiple usage scenarios applied to the same dataset. The results demonstrate that assessment outcomes adapt meaningfully to different intended uses, while feasibility-gated execution reduces unrealistic or non-executable rule generation. The proposed approach provides a practical foundation for deploying autonomous yet controlled data quality assessment in modern data-driven environments.

</details>


### [175] [A Multimodal Machine Learning Framework for Enterprise Database Workload-Aware Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13699)
*Ruchi Pakhle,Siddhant Pawar*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态机器学习框架，通过融合工作负载特征、系统遥测数据和操作信号，对企业数据库事件进行根因分析。实验表明，LightGBM模型在可解释性和预测性能之间取得了最佳平衡，验证了结合多模态遥测与工作负载上下文构建自动化、可解释的根因分析系统的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据库事件的根因分析通常依赖人工检查日志、性能指标和工作负载行为，耗时且效率低下。现有方法往往只关注单一证据来源，无法捕捉CPU饱和、I/O瓶颈、锁竞争、死锁和慢查询等事件背后的完整操作上下文。

Method: 提出了一种多模态机器学习框架，结合工作负载特征、系统遥测数据以及计算、存储和加速器导向数据集的操作信号。设计工作负载感知特征用于分类工作负载行为，并支持下游根因诊断。评估了随机森林、LightGBM和前馈神经网络三种模型。

Result: 工作负载感知的特征工程提高了工作负载的可分离性。LightGBM在预测性能和可解释性之间取得了最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 将多模态遥测数据与工作负载上下文相结合，可以为自动化且可解释的根因分析系统提供实用的基础。

Abstract: Root cause analysis for enterprise database incidents is often a manual and time consuming process that requires operators to inspect logs, performance metrics, and workload behavior. Existing approaches commonly focus on a single source of evidence, which limits their ability to capture the broader operational context behind incidents such as CPU saturation, I/O bottlenecks, lock contention, deadlocks, and slow query execution.
  This paper presents a multimodal machine learning framework for workload-aware root cause analysis in enterprise database environments. The proposed approach combines workload characteristics, system telemetry, and operational signals from compute, storage, and accelerator oriented datasets. Engineered workload aware features are used to classify workload behavior and support downstream diagnosis of likely incident causes.
  The framework evaluates Random Forest, LightGBM, and feedforward neural network models for workload classification and root cause analysis support. Experimental results show that workload aware feature engineering improves workload separability, with LightGBM providing the strongest balance of predictive performance and interpretability. The results suggest that combining multimodal telemetry with workload context can provide a practical foundation for automated and explainable root cause analysis systems.

</details>


### [176] [Towards an open registry of Earth observation instruments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13923)
*David Montero,César Aybar,Miguel D. Mahecha,Luis Gómez-Chova*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了"Awesome Earth Observation Instruments"——一个开放、标准化、社区导向的地球观测仪器注册目录，提供机器可读的仪器元数据，解决了现有目录仅描述数据产品而缺乏对仪器本身持久元数据管理的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管地球观测（EO）仪器数量激增，但用户缺乏一个可靠、统一的来源来获取这些仪器的存在和关键特征信息；现有数据目录主要描述数据产品，而非提供关于生产这些数据产品的仪器的持久、可维护的元数据。

Method: 在GitHub上构建开放目录，采用轻量核心+模块化扩展的通用模式（涵盖光谱、几何、数据访问相关元数据），通过自动化模式验证+人工审核保证质量，支持版本化、可扩展的开放式演进，并计划提供API接口以支持编程访问和互操作性。

Result: 建立了存储地球观测仪器元数据的开放注册目录，支持社区贡献、自动化验证和人工审查，实现了标准化与灵活性的平衡，可随新仪器和新需求持续演进。

Conclusion: 该开放仪器目录有助于促进地球观测数据的发现、解读和分析，通过提供仪器层面的持久元数据来弥补现有数据目录的不足，并支持未来通过API与常见EO分析环境集成。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) is essential to understanding the Earth system, enabling the transformation of planetary properties into measurable variables that can be analysed, compared, and modelled. In recent decades, EO capabilities have grown rapidly, accompanied by an even faster expansion in the number and variety of available EO instruments. Today, EO includes instruments deployed on satellites, airborne platforms, and terrestrial or in-situ systems. However, despite this proliferation of instruments, users often lack a single, reliable source describing their existence and key characteristics. Although existing data catalogues have substantially improved dataset discovery, they primarily describe data products rather than providing persistent, curated metadata about the instruments that produced them. Here we present Awesome Earth Observation Instruments, an open, standardized, and community-oriented registry providing machine-readable metadata for EO instruments. The catalogue is hosted on GitHub and allows contributors to submit instrument metadata following a common schema. The schema combines a lightweight core with modular extensions covering spectral, geometric, and data access-related metadata, enabling both standardization and flexibility across diverse EO systems. All submissions undergo automated schema validation and human review. Because the schema is open, versioned, and extensible, the catalogue can continuously evolve as new instruments and metadata requirements emerge. This facilitates the discovery, interpretation, and analysis of EO data in light of instrument characteristics. To support programmatic access and interoperability, we further envisage an API for integration within common EO analysis environments. The catalogue is openly available at https://github.com/awesome-spectral-indices/awesome-earth-observation-instruments.

</details>


### [177] [Vivace: Exact Temporal OLAP over Interval Histories via Independent Serverless Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14069)
*Woohyeok Park,Taeyoon Kim,Hyunjoon Kim,Kungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Vivace是一个用于区间历史数据的无服务器时序OLAP系统。它通过查询前的布局步骤（复制跨越边界的区间以确保每个函数完整读取数据）和查询时的合并步骤（按算子特定规则组合部分结果），解决了无服务器函数执行时序分析时数据不完整和聚合错误两个问题。在AWS Lambda上的实验表明，相比直接查询历史的SQL基线，延迟和成本分别降低82%和84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SQL引擎和无服务器分析系统无法同时解决无服务器函数执行时序OLAP时的两个问题：函数可能无法接收到决定其时间范围状态的行，以及对持续时间加权和累积阈值查询进行简单求和会得到错误结果。

Method: 采用两阶段方案：1) 查询前布局步骤，通过复制跨越边界的区间来分区区间历史，使每个函数从单个文件中完整计算其时间范围；2) 查询时合并步骤，按算子特定规则组合部分结果——关联聚合合并中间值，排序在每个时间范围内重新排序候选者。

Result: 在AWS Lambda上使用真实数据集，相比直接查询历史的SQL基线，延迟降低82%，货币成本降低84%。

Conclusion: 证明了这种分区执行在规范形式上与单进程计算等价，并展示了Vivace在准确时序OLAP上的鲁棒通用性和高效性。

Abstract: Temporal online analytical processing (OLAP) analyzes past states of data whose values change over time. Such histories are naturally stored as interval histories, in which each row records the period during which a value remained valid. Because temporal analyses typically arrive in infrequent, intermittent bursts, serverless execution that launches functions only at query time offers a cost advantage over always-on clusters. Splitting a computation that a single process performs as a whole across independent serverless functions, however, breaks correctness in two ways. A function may not receive the rows that determine the state of its time range, and naively summing partial results yields incorrect answers for duration-weighted and cumulative-threshold queries. Existing SQL engines and serverless analytics do not address both problems together.
  This paper presents Vivace, a serverless system for exact temporal OLAP over interval histories. Vivace resolves the two problems in separate stages. Before any query arrives, a pre-query layout step partitions the interval history, replicating boundary-crossing intervals so each function computes its range completely from a single file. At query time, a merge step combines partial results under operator-specific rules. Associative aggregates merge intermediate values, and ranking re-orders candidates within each time range. We prove that this partitioned execution matches single-process computation up to canonical form. Evaluated on AWS Lambda with real-world datasets, Vivace reduces latency and monetary cost by up to 82% and 84%, respectively, against an equivalent SQL baseline that queries the history directly, demonstrating robust generality and efficiency.

</details>


### [178] [Revisiting Filtered ANN Benchmarks: A Hardness-Controlled Benchmark Generator for Realistic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14193)
*Mintaek Lim,Dogeun Kim,Minwoo Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出 α-Hardness 度量指标，用于量化过滤近似最近邻（FANN）搜索中查询的复杂程度，并基于此设计 HCBGen 基准生成器，解决现有混合工作负载基准缺乏真实性和难度控制的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FANN 评估依赖于临时合成或半真实数据集，缺乏对真实混合工作负载的忠实模拟，导致评估结果不可靠。核心问题在于现有基准忽略了执行驱动型查询难度的关键作用——早期过滤失败会触发过度获取候选结果，从而影响延迟、吞吐量和召回率。

Method: 1) 提出 α-Hardness 查询级硬度度量，基于过度获取因子（over-fetch factor）建模条件执行链，并扩展到策略条件设置；2) 设计 HCBGen 基准生成器，以 α-Hardness 为控制信号，在粗粒度偏置模式下合成工作负载或匹配目标硬度分布。

Result: 1) α-Hardness 与经验性能呈现强单调对齐，而选择性或属性-向量相关性等常见代理指标频繁不稳定或不一致；2) 现有基准仅覆盖硬度谱中较窄且容易的部分，掩盖了高硬度查询下的鲁棒性差距；3) 匹配硬度分布可实现隐私保护的代理工作负载，紧密复现性能趋势。

Conclusion: α-Hardness 是评估 FANN 搜索的有效硬度度量，HCBGen 能生成更真实的混合工作负载。通过控制硬度分布，可在不泄露原始数据的情况下构建代理基准，弥合研究基准与真实评估之间的差距。

Abstract: Filtered approximate nearest neighbor (FANN) search must satisfy both vector similarity and structured predicates, yet evaluations remain brittle because real hybrid workloads are rarely shareable and existing benchmarks rely on ad-hoc synthetic or semi-real constructions. We argue that realism hinges on execution-driven query difficulty: failures in early filtering trigger over-fetching of additional candidates, shaping latency, throughput, and recall. Building on this insight, we propose $α$-Hardness, a query-level hardness metric that models the conditional execution chain via the over-fetch factor and extends naturally to strategy-conditioned settings. Across diverse datasets and hybrid strategies, $α$-Hardness exhibits strong monotonic alignment with empirical performance, while common proxies such as selectivity or attribute-vector correlation are frequently unstable or strategy-inconsistent. We further introduce HCBGen, a hardness-controlled benchmark generator that uses $α$-Hardness as an explicit control signal to synthesize workloads under coarse bias modes or to match a target hardness profile. Our experiments show that widely used benchmarks occupy a narrow, relatively easy portion of the hardness spectrum, masking robustness gaps that emerge under harder queries. Finally, we demonstrate that matching hardness distributions enables privacy-preserving proxy workloads that closely reproduce performance trends, bridging research benchmarks and real evaluation.

</details>


### [179] [TACO: A Benchmark for Open-Domain Text-to-SQL with Ambiguous and Cross-Database Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14201)
*Chao Deng,Ju Fan,Yuyu Luo,Qinliang Xue,Meihao Fan,Yuxin Zhang,Min Zhang,Xiaofeng Jia,Jing Zhang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: TACO是一个面向开放域文本到SQL的基准数据集，包含1,500个真实世界示例和13,000个合成示例，覆盖交通、医疗、金融等多个领域，并提出了基线方法TACO-SQL，实验表明现有方法在开放域文本到SQL任务上仍与人类存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到SQL基准主要针对封闭域场景，具有预定义数据库模式和明确的问题，无法处理开放域场景中的模糊问题、未指定数据库和跨数据库查询等挑战。为填补这一空白，作者提出了TACO基准。

Method: 构建了基于智慧城市数据服务的1,500个真实示例和基于大规模开放数据门户生成的13,000个高质量合成示例，开发了保持真实查询复杂度的数据合成流水线，并提出了由问题重写、表格链接和查询规划组成的基线方法TACO-SQL。

Result: 在TACO上对多种最近文本到SQL方法进行实验，TACO-SQL取得了最佳结果，但现有方法与人写SQL之间仍存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 开放域文本到SQL任务具有挑战性，TACO作为有价值的基准将推动未来研究。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL aims to translate natural language questions into executable SQL queries over structured databases. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on closed-domain settings with predefined database schemas and well-specified questions, but they fall short in addressing the challenges of open-domain scenarios, such as ambiguous questions, unspecified databases, and cross-database querying. To bridge this gap, we introduce TACO, a benchmark for open-domain Text-to-SQL with Ambiguous and Cross-database queries. TACO consists of 1,500 real-world Text-to-SQL examples based on a smart city data service and 13,000 high-quality synthetic examples generated based on large-scale open data portals, covering diverse domains such as transportation, healthcare, and finance. To construct the synthetic examples, we develop an effective data synthesis pipeline that preserves the complexity of real-world queries. To demonstrate the utility of TACO, we introduce a baseline TACO-SQL composed of question rewriting, table linking, and query planning, to illustrate the challenges posed by TACO and to better understand the limitations of existing Text-to-SQL approaches. Extensive experiments on TACO using a variety of recent Text-to-SQL approaches show that, while TACO-SQL achieves the best results, a significant gap still remains between the existing approaches and human-written SQL. These findings highlight the difficulty of open-domain Text-to-SQL and position TACO as a valuable benchmark to drive future research.

</details>


### [180] [WikiKV: Schema-Evolving Path-Indexed Storage for Hierarchical Knowledge Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14275)
*Feifei Li,Haoliang Ming,Zihan Li,Hang Liao,Xingyu Fan,Xiaoqing Wu,Chenggong Wang,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: WikiKV是一种面向层次化知识库 workloads 的路径索引键值存储模型，通过意图锚定模式归纳、连续演化一致性协议和预算化导航算子，在 WeChat 和 AuthTrace 数据集上实现了优于关系型、图型和文件系统后端的低延迟与高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的层次化知识库（如树状维基）广泛应用于检索增强应用，但其存储层仍被视为实现细节。现有存储模型无法同时满足层次化、查询密集和持续演化这三个核心特性。

Method: WikiKV包含三个组件：(i) 基于意图锚定模式归纳的数据驱动schema，及连续演化算子；(ii) 路径索引存储模型的一致性协议，在并发离线重写下避免部分读且无需读路径锁定；(iii) 预算化导航算子，通过搜索加速路由将LLM辅助下降步数从d降至O(1)，同时保留随时语义。

Result: 在微信公众平台AI助手实际部署及AuthTrace数据集上，WikiKV在四个查询算子中达到平衡的低延迟，优于关系型、图型和文件系统后端；端到端答案正确率63.2%，超过多个RAG基线，在低/高扇入多文档问题上差距更大。

Conclusion: WikiKV专门为层次化知识库workload设计的路径索引存储模型是有效的，其各组件在消融实验中均被证实有效，为检索增强应用提供了更优的存储基础设施。

Abstract: LLM-curated hierarchical knowledge bases, namely a tree-structured wiki whose nodes summarize an underlying corpus, have become a dominant substrate for retrieval-augmented applications, yet their storage layer is still treated as an implementation detail. This workload is hierarchical, query-intensive, and continuously evolving, and no existing storage model natively captures all three properties at once. We present WikiKV, a path-indexed key-value storage model purpose-built for this workload, comprising three components: (i) a data-driven schema that bootstraps the hierarchy via Intent-Anchored Schema Induction and refines it through Continuous Evolution Operators; (ii) a consistency protocol for the path-indexed storage model that precludes partial-read observations under concurrent offline rewrites without read-path locking; and (iii) a budgeted navigation operator whose search-accelerated routing reduces the expected number of LLM-assisted descent steps from d to O(1) while preserving anytime semantics with progressively refined answers. We evaluate WikiKV through real-world deployment for the WeChat Official Account AI Assistant and benchmark it against diverse baselines on the AuthTrace dataset, where it achieves balanced low per-operator latency across four query operators against relational, graph, and FS backends, and reaches 63.2% end-to-end answer correctness, exceeding multiple RAG baselines, with the gap widening on low- and high-fan-in multi-document questions. Ablation study further confirms the effectiveness of WikiKV's components.

</details>


### [181] [Transforming Shape Schemas with Composable Property-Graph Queries (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14309)
*Philipp Seifer,Daniel Hernández,Ralf Lämmel,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过映射到RDF/SHACL和SPARQL CONSTRUCT，利用描述逻辑推理器对属性图进行模式推断的方法，解决了在给定输入模式和G-CORE查询条件下推断输出模式的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性图查询（G-CORE）对图进行变换后，需要保证输出图符合某种模式约束，但现有工作仅针对SPARQL CONSTRUCT查询，属性图因其标签、属性注解和一等公民边等特性，无法直接应用描述逻辑进行模式推断。

Method: 构建三层映射体系：1) 将属性图重化为RDF；2) 将ProGS模式映射为SHACL；3) 将G-CORE查询映射为SPARQL CONSTRUCT。通过这一映射层，利用现有高效的描述逻辑推理器来推断输出模式，并建立了映射的模式约束合理性和语义等价性的元理论。

Result: 提出了不依赖图实例的模式推断流程，确保输出模式适用于所有符合输入模式的输入图产生的输出图，并证明了推断出的模式约束的合理性以及映射后模式和查询的语义等价性。

Conclusion: 通过额外的映射层将属性图模式推断问题转化为描述逻辑推理问题，使得该问题变得可处理，为属性图查询的静态模式保证提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Property graphs may be constrained by schemas that inform both query engines and human users about the shape of valid data, enforcing a contract between data provider and consumer. Composable property-graph queries transform input graphs into output graphs. Then, the question arises of which schema can be expected after one (or several) transformation steps. We investigate how schema constraints can be inferred given an input schema and a transforming query. Specifically, we propose a reasoning procedure that, given an input schema in ProGS and a query in G-CORE infers an output schema. Since graph updates will happen frequently, our inference procedure does not rely on graph instances, such that the computed output schema applies to all graphs originating from any input graph complying with the input schema. Related work has addressed this problem for SPARQL CONSTRUCT queries, encoding it in Description Logics (DLs) so that the output schema is entailed by axioms inferred from input schema and queries. Property graphs and their queries, however, complicate the matter, as property graphs feature label and property annotations as well as first-class edges. Thus, reification has to be used in one way or another, though available DLs lack the means to encode such features directly. We approach this novel challenge via a family of mappings for i) property graphs reified in RDF, aligned with ii) a mapping from ProGS to SHACL and iii) a mapping from G-CORE to SPARQL CONSTRUCT queries. In this manner, schema inference for property graphs becomes manageable, as we break apart the problem through the extra mapping layer and utilize efficient DL reasoners. We develop the metatheory regarding the soundness of inferred schema constraints and the semantic equivalence of mapped schemas and queries.

</details>


### [182] [PLRTune: Importance Pre-Sampling and LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Automatic Database Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14312)
*Xinyue Yang,Chen Zheng,Yaoyang Hou,Renhao Zhang,Yinyan Zhang,Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PLRTune提出了一种分阶段数据库调优系统，通过工作负载特定的领域知识识别简化搜索空间，并利用执行引导的提示精炼和TD3后调优阶段，在MySQL和PostgreSQL上实现了平均9.50%的性能提升，且调优速度比最强基线快9.03倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动数据库调优方法面临高维旋钮空间、在线调优成本高、LLM或社区文档提供的文本提示不可靠，以及初始化后难以进一步挖掘优化空间等挑战。

Method: 提出三阶段调优系统：(1) 重要性预采样与重排序策略，以工作负载特定方式识别主导旋钮子集并导出紧凑状态表示；(2) 执行引导的提示精炼技术，改善文档引导调优的初始化质量；(3) 后调优精炼阶段，利用TD3算法探索主导旋钮子集并进一步挖掘剩余优化空间。

Result: 在MySQL和PostgreSQL上，PLRTune在所有测试工作负载下均取得最佳最终结果，平均优于最优替代方案9.50%，且达到最强基线最佳性能水平的速度平均快9.03倍。

Conclusion: PLRTune通过工作负载特定的领域知识缩小搜索空间并分阶段优化，在保证最终调优质量的同时显著提升了调优效率。

Abstract: Configuration tuning is critical to database performance, yet automatic database tuning remains challenging due to high-dimensional knob spaces, substantial online tuning cost, unreliable textual hints derived from Large Language Models (LLMs) or community documents, and the difficulty of exploiting the remaining optimization room after initialization.
  Hence, we propose PLRTune, a staged database tuning system that leverages workload-specific domain knowledge to identify a reduced search space and further optimize within this promising region. First, we develop an importance pre-sampling and reranking strategy to identify the dominant knob subset in a workload-specific manner and derive a compact state representation. Second, we design an execution-guided hint refinement technique to improve the initialization quality of documentation-guided tuning. Finally, we propose a post-tuning refinement stage that leverages Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to explore the dominant knob subset and further exploit the remaining optimization room.
  We evaluate PLRTune on MySQL and PostgreSQL across diverse benchmark workloads. Compared with state-of-the-art approaches, PLRTune achieves the best final result on all tested workloads, improving over the corresponding best-performing alternative by 9.50% on average. Moreover, PLRTune reaches the strongest baseline's best performance level 9.03 times faster on average across workloads, demonstrating its practical runtime efficiency without sacrificing final tuning quality.

</details>


### [183] [Evaluating and Generating Query Workloads for High Dimensional Vector Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14511)
*Matteo Ceccarello,Alexandra Levchenko,Ioana Ileana,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对相似性搜索基准测试中工作负载难度缺乏控制的问题，提出了两种查询工作负载合成方法HephAnn和HephGrad，能够按用户指定的难度目标生成测试数据，从而更深入地评估算法行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相似性搜索算法的基准测试中，工作负载的难度缺乏有效控制，导致不同方法之间的比较不够公平和全面。需要一种能够量化查询难度并按需生成不同难度工作负载的方法。

Method: 首先评估多种查询难度度量指标在捕捉查询经验性难度（即索引结构为提供答案所投入的计算代价）方面的能力。然后提出两种合成查询工作负载的方法：HephAnn（对目标难度度量假设更少）和HephGrad（速度更快），两者均能生成满足用户指定难度目标的工作负载。

Result: 两种方法均能生成具有期望难度的工作负载。HephGrad在速度上更优，而HephAnn对目标难度度量的假设条件更为宽松。

Conclusion: 所生成的不同难度的工作负载可用于深入理解相似性搜索算法的行为特征，为算法评估提供更系统、可控的基准测试工具。

Abstract: Similarity search lies at the heart of many modern applications, ranging from databases to deep learning to data series analysis. As such, a vast effort has been invested in developing algorithms, data structures and implementations to speed up this crucial subroutine. To empirically validate these approaches, several benchmarking efforts have been initiated covering a wide array of datasets. In this paper, we observe that usually little control is exercised on the hardness of the workloads with which methods are tested and compared. To address this issue, we first evaluate several query hardness measures with respect to their ability to capture the empirical hardness of a query, i.e. the effort invested by an index data structure to provide an answer. Then, we propose two methods, deemed \HephAnn and \HephGrad, for synthesizing query workloads so that they meet a user-specified hardness target. Both methods allow to produce workloads with the desired hardness: we find that \HephGrad is faster, while \HephAnn makes fewer assumptions on the target hardness measure. The resulting workloads can be used to gain insights into the behavior of similarity search algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Do programming languages still matter to your AI coding agent teammate? Evidence at scale from chess engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13763)
*Mathieu Acher,Jean-Marc Jézéquel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿编码代理（Claude Code和Codex）在多语言环境下生成完整软件系统的能力，通过构建34个国际象棋引擎（覆盖17种编程语言）进行实证分析。结果表明代理确实具有多语言能力，但语言选择仍然显著影响系统性能、成本和工程特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究两个核心问题：1) AI编码代理是否能够在任意目标语言中编程，包括那些没有可比开源先例的语言；2) 语言选择是否仍会影响生成的人工制品，以及沿着哪些维度产生影响。

Method: 采用多语言案例研究方法，构建国际象棋引擎作为测试系统。使用两个前沿代理（Claude Code和Codex），在无象棋知识或实现指导的情况下，依据记录的干预和停止策略进行实验。代理生成了34个引擎，覆盖17种主流、专业、遗留和深奥编程语言。综合运用每引擎特征分析、独立Elo评估、会话轨迹分析以及代码和转录本的定性分析。

Result: 1) 每种尝试的语言都产生了至少一个功能丰富的可用引擎，包括无开源先例的语言（如LaTeX）；2) 代码为从头合成而非复制；3) 强棋力仅在主流编译语言中可达；4) 成本和工程工作量随语言异质程度急剧增长；5) 特征选择在不同语言家族间存在差异；6) 代理会主动验证自己的工作，但其强度自我估计存在偏差；7) 少数引擎通过调用国际象棋库"作弊"。

Conclusion: 编程语言不再是AI能否构建工作系统的问题，而是关乎性能、成本、构建内容以及需要多少人工监督验证的问题。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents now promise end-to-end authorship of complete software systems. Two empirical questions follow: can AI coding-agent teammates program in any target language, including ones with no comparable prior open-source artefact? If so, does language choice still shape the artefact, and along which dimensions? We study both through a polyglot case study built around chess engines: non-trivial multi-component systems that admit a hierarchy of language-agnostic oracles, from exact move-generation correctness to a strength scale (Elo), observable from Rust to Brainfuck. We prompted two frontier agents (Claude Code and Codex) at the capability level, without chess knowledge or implementation guidance, under a documented intervention and stopping policy. The agents produced 34 chess engines spanning 17 primary programming languages, from mainstream to specialised, domain-specific, legacy, and esoteric targets. We combine per-engine feature analysis, independent Elo assessment, and session trajectories with qualitative analysis of code and transcripts.
  Frontier coding agents are genuinely polyglot: every language we tried produced at least one feature-rich working engine, several with no prior open-source counterpart of comparable scope (e.g., LaTeX), and the code is synthesised from scratch rather than copied. Yet language choice still matters: strong playing strength is only reachable in mainstream compiled languages, cost and engineering effort grow sharply as the language becomes more exotic, and feature choices shift across language families. Agents validate their own work unprompted, but their strength self-estimates are biased and a few engines cheated by calling a chess library. Programming language is no longer about whether AI teammates can build a working system, but about performance, cost, what gets built, and how much human supervision validation still needs.

</details>


### [185] [I'm Sorry Driver, I'm Afraid I Can't Do That: Appraising the Safety of LLMs within Automotive Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14327)
*Shaun Feakins,Ibrahim Habli,Kim Littler,Robert Palin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了当前AI开发中将LLM集成到汽车控制任务中的安全保证框架，发现这些框架在实时安全关键场景中存在概念性和具体性挑战，并通过案例研究和安全论证指出了现有解决方案的局限性，同时提出了面向未来的保障机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在汽车领域的快速集成，如何确保其在安全关键的控制任务中的可靠性成为亟待解决的问题。现有框架面临上游通用模型与下游特定车辆架构之间的双重挑战，亟需系统性的安全评估。

Method: 通过概念分析识别上游通用模型与下游部署的双重挑战；结合ISO21448（延迟等工程约束）和ISO/PAS8800（对齐问题等LLM特有议题）等现有标准进行具体挑战分析；以Talk2Drive开源仓库为例进行实验性案例研究；构建安全论证以明确现有解决方案的局限性。

Result: 现有框架在实时安全关键场景中效果有限；存在延迟等基础工程约束以及LLM特有的对齐问题等双重挑战；案例研究验证了上述问题的实际存在。

Conclusion: 尽管存在诸多局限性，LLM在汽车领域的应用仍在技术层面推进和运营化；提出了针对LLM相关危险事件的潜在保障机制。

Abstract: This paper appraises recent frameworks within AI development to integrate LLMs into control tasks in automotive contexts from the perspective of safety assurance. This work has built upon the rapid integration of LLMs across automotive settings. However, we find that at present, these frameworks face significant challenges, limiting their efficacy in real-time safety-critical contexts. Firstly, we consider conceptual challenges, including the fact that deployers are faced with a dual challenge, wherein they must assure a model which has been developed upstream, i.e. as general-purpose tools by the large AI labs, in a downstream context, i.e. into specific vehicle architectures. Secondly, we consider concrete challenges from across existing standards. We show that there are currently both fundamental engineering constraints covered in ISO21448, such as latency, and novel LLM-specific issues, such as alignment-related issues covered in ISO/PAS8800. We ground both examples in a concrete introductory, experimental case study exploring an existing open-source repository, Talk2Drive. We present a safety argument in order to make explicit the limitations of existing solutions. Nonetheless, given that the use of LLMs in automotive contexts is being explored at a technical level and operationalised, we propose potential assurance mechanisms for LLM-related hazardous events going forward.

</details>


### [186] [A Benchmark and Framework for Evaluating Next Action Predictions in Spreadsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13802)
*Tejas Agrawal,Vu Le,Sumit Gulwani,Gust Verbruggen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对电子表格中缺乏预测性代码补全功能的问题，提出了一个用于预测用户未来操作的基准测试，通过人工策展的52个动作序列和在线评估方法，验证了多种基线预测模型（包括零样本LLM、微调SLM和经典模型）的性能，并分析了保存动作、误报、效率、用户画像、触发条件和上下文影响等关键属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格软件中几乎不存在预测性代码补全功能，而该功能在传统编程环境中已显著提升开发效率。为填补这一空白，需要建立一个能够观察用户操作序列并预测未来动作的系统。

Method: (1) 人工策展52个序列（共12K个动作），通过参数化启发式搜索和LLM精炼从公开语料库重构电子表格；(2) 提出在线评估方法：每次用户操作后给出预测，接受或拒绝该预测，接受则更新未来动作，重复直至得到目标电子表格。

Result: 使用多种基线预测器（零样本LLM、微调SLM和经典模型）进行评测，分析了保存动作与误报的性质、效率、用户画像影响、触发条件影响和上下文影响等属性。

Conclusion: 该基准测试为电子表格中的动作预测系统提供了标准化评估框架，揭示了多种因素对预测性能的影响，为未来电子表格智能补全功能的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Predictive code completion greatly accelerates how quickly developers work. In spreadsheets, despite being much more common, such auto-completion features are virtually non-existent. To address this gap, we introduce a benchmark for systems that observe a sequence of user actions in a spreadsheet and predict future actions. Two challenges are (1) the absence of edit histories in public spreadsheet corpora and (2) the complex space of spreadsheet actions (spatial, temporal, composite). To address (1), we manually curate 52 sequences of 12K actions that recreate spreadsheets from public corpora, seeded by parametrized heuristics and LLM refinement. To address (2), we propose an online evaluation that expects a prediction after each user action, accepts or rejects that prediction, updates the future actions upon acceptance, and repeats this until the target spreadsheet is obtained. We use multiple baseline predictors (including zero-shot LLMs, fine-tuned SLMs, and classical models) and analyze different properties that our benchmark teaches us, including but not limited to: properties of saved actions and false positives, efficiency, effect of user profiles, effect of triggers, and effect of context.

</details>


### [187] [An Empirical Study of Gemini 3 for Detecting Natural Language Test Smells in Manual Test Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13804)
*Keila Lucas,Rohit Gheyi,Márcio Ribeiro,Fabio Palomba,Luana Martins,Elvys Soares*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究使用大型语言模型（GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW）通过基于提示的完整测试用例分析策略，检测自然语言手动测试用例中的测试坏味。实验表明，该方法在100个Ubuntu测试用例上优于之前的小型语言模型（GEMMA-3-4B、LLAMA-3.2-3B、PHI-4-14B），并能生成可操作的解释。研究发现测试坏味普遍存在，平均每步检测到近一个坏味。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动测试用例存在测试坏味（如歧义、冗余、缺少检查），影响可靠性、可维护性和可复现性。现有检测方法依赖人工规则，难以泛化和扩展。作者旨在探索使用大语言模型进行更有效、可扩展的自动检测。

Method: 采用基于提示的完整测试用例分析策略，使用GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW对100个Ubuntu手动测试用例进行七种测试坏味类型的检测，并与之前评估的小型语言模型（GEMMA-3-4B、LLAMA-3.2-3B、PHI-4-14B）进行性能比较。

Result: GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW在检测性能上优于所有被比较的小型语言模型，并能生成可操作的解释。测试坏味在实际中普遍存在，平均每个测试步骤检测到近一个坏味。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的完整测试用例分析策略可有效检测手动测试用例中的测试坏味，为手动测试工件的自动化质量保障提供了可行方案，凸显了对可扩展自动化质量支持的需求。

Abstract: Manual testing, in which testers follow natural language instructions to validate system behavior, remains essential for uncovering issues that are difficult to capture with automation. However, manual test cases often contain test smells, quality issues such as ambiguity, redundancy, or missing checks that reduce reliability, maintainability, and reproducibility. Existing detection approaches largely depend on manually engineered rules and thus struggle to generalize and scale across heterogeneous test suites. In our previous work, we assessed the feasibility of using Small Language Models (SLMs) for test smell detection by evaluating GEMMA-3-4B, LLAMA-3.2-3B, and PHI-4-14B on test steps from 143 real-world Ubuntu test cases, covering seven smell types. PHI-4-14B achieved the best performance. In this article, we investigate whether a contemporary Large Language Model (GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW) available at the time of the study can identify test smells in natural language manual test cases using a prompt-based, whole-test-case analysis strategy. Unlike approaches that analyze individual test steps in isolation, our approach evaluates complete test cases, enabling the model to consider relationships and dependencies among test steps. We evaluate the approach on 100 Ubuntu test cases covering seven test smell types and compare its performance against previously evaluated SLMs, including GEMMA-3-4B, LLAMA-3.2-3B, and PHI-4-14B. Our results show that GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW outperforms the SLMs, while producing actionable explanations that can help practitioners revise manual test cases for greater clarity and consistency. We also find that test smells are pervasive in practice, with nearly one detected test smell per step on average, highlighting the need for scalable and automated quality support for manual testing artifacts.

</details>


### [188] [A Principled Framework for Safe Algorithm Updates in Automated Insulin Delivery Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13882)
*Thomas Screven,Ziqiang "Joe" Zhu,Deniz Cengiz,Rayhan A. Lal,Korey K. Hood,Samuel T. King*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于评估AID算法更新安全性的双部分框架，包括对bug进行分类（事实性、启发式、计算性）以及临床等效性评估。通过将Trio的oref算法从Javascript移植到Swift，并在736,480次调用和多种仿真中验证，结果显示两种实现在Time in Range（84.9% vs. 84.9%）等指标上几乎一致，超过99%的配对血糖值落在Parkes误差网格A/B区，达到了临床等效阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共同适应系统中，用户已经围绕现有算法行为调整了设置，因此AID算法的bug修复可能反而破坏血糖控制。目前缺乏一个原则性框架来评估AID算法更新的安全性。

Method: 提出双部分框架：1) 将bug分类为事实性、启发式、计算性三类，分别对应不同管理策略；2) 通过配对血糖值误差分析评估临床等效性。采用影子执行（736,480次调用）和机械性in silico仿真与数据驱动回放仿真进行验证。

Result: Swift与JavaScript实现机械性in silico仿真：Time in Range均为84.9%，Glycemia Risk Index分别为23.5% vs 23.9%，>99%配对血糖在Parkes误差网格A/B区。影子执行中oref组件不匹配率极低（iob 0.43%, autosens 1.22%, determineBasal 0.07%, meal 0.01%），仅0.03%的iob调用存在临床意义差异。数据驱动回放仿真中>99%下游配对血糖在Parkes A/B区。

Conclusion: 该框架将bug修复原则与多方法临床评估相结合，可用于评估AID算法更新的安全性。框架是系统无关的，适用于所有广泛使用的开源AID系统，案例研究强调需要对事实性和计算性bug进行系统性修复。

Abstract: Background: AID algorithms require ongoing software updates and bug fixes. In co-adapted systems, where users tune settings around existing algorithmic behavior, bug fixes can paradoxically disrupt glycemic control. No principled framework evaluates the safety of AID algorithm updates.
  Methods: Our two-part framework classifies bugs and evaluates the clinical equivalence of AID system software updates. Bugs are classified as factual, heuristic, or computational, each with distinct management strategies. Classifications were validated from porting Trio's oref algorithm from Javascript to a bug-fixed Swift implementation. We compared implementations using shadow execution on 736,480 invocations from eight Trio users. The second component assesses clinical equivalence with error analysis on paired glucose values, applied to both Trio implementations using mechanistic in silico and data-driven replay simulation.
  Results: In mechanistic in silico simulation, the Swift and Javascript implementations produced nearly identical Time in Range (84.9% vs. 84.9%) and Glycemia Risk Index (23.5% vs. 23.9%), with more than 99% of paired glucose in Parkes Error Grid Zones A and B, meeting our clinical equivalence threshold. Shadow execution showed low mismatch rates in oref components (iob 0.43%, autosens 1.22%, determineBasal 0.07%, meal 0.01%), with clinically meaningful differences in 0.03% of iob invocations. Data-driven replay simulations of bugs revealed more than 99% of downstream paired glucose in Parkes Error Grid Zones A and B, also meeting our clinical equivalence threshold.
  Conclusions: Our framework integrates bug-fixing principles with multi-method clinical evaluation to assess AID algorithm update safety. It is system-agnostic and applicable to all widely used OS-AID systems, with case studies highlighting the need for systematic remediation of factual and computational bugs.

</details>


### [189] [Bayesian-Calibrated Detection of Hallucinated Package Imports in AI-Assisted Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13918)
*Lom M. Hillah,Jean-Marc Richard,Ryan Hasnaoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于贝叶斯校准的层，用于增强LLM代码中恶意包导入检测（slopsquat检测器）。该层通过3类别认知分类法（经验校准、构造论证、工程判断追踪）生成Beta后验概率，并利用PyPI元数据信号（包年龄、发布次数等）捕获注册但可疑的包。在1,734个Python片段上评估，展示了良好的校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有slopsquat检测器仅输出二元决策（标记/不标记），缺乏概率化风险评估能力，且无法利用元数据信号识别注册但可疑的包，而这正是LLM代码攻击的实际场景。

Method: 提出贝叶斯校准层，基于3类别认知分类法（经验校准、构造论证、工程判断追踪）对每个先验进行分类，输出Beta后验概率；在200/404注册表通道外，利用PyPI元数据信号（包年龄、发布次数、作者描述、摘要）检测注册但可疑的包；使用McNemar配对检验评估检测器不对称性，用期望校准误差（ECE）和Brier分数评估校准性能。

Result: 在1,734个Python片段（189-prompt BigCodeBench切片 + 100-prompt小众库压力测试集）上评估，涵盖6个模型（4个云端模型+2个本地模型）。校准层复现了严格注册表检测结果，并在元数据通道上引入了良好校准的额外标记。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯校准层为下游CI门控提供了可直接使用的风险感知原语，支持基于原则的检测阈值决策，优于纯二元的注册表基线方法。

Abstract: We present a Bayesian calibration layer for slopsquat detectors -- those that flag hallucinated package imports in code produced by large language models (LLMs). Where existing pipelines emit binary decisions (flag / do-not-flag), our layer emits a Beta-posterior probability per detection, derived from a 3-category epistemic taxonomy that explicitly classifies each prior as empirically calibrated, constructively argued, or engineering-judgement-traced. Beyond the primary 200/404 registry channel, the calibrated layer exploits PyPI metadata signals -- package age, release count, author descriptor, summary -- to surface registered-but-suspicious packages that a binary registry detector misses, which is the realistic post-LLM-emission attacker regime. The resulting risk-aware primitive is directly consumable by downstream CI gates and supports principled threshold decisions across detection rules. We evaluate the calibration on a merged corpus of 1,734 Python snippets -- a stratified 189-prompt BigCodeBench slice plus a 100-prompt niche-library stress-test set, generated across a six-model panel spanning four cloud models (Claude-Sonnet-4.6, Mistral-Large, DeepSeek-v4-pro, DeepSeek-R1) and two local open-weight code models (Mistral Codestral, Meta CodeLlama). Against a re-implemented binary baseline inspired by Mahmud et al. -- which shares its registry oracle with our ground truth and therefore serves as a degenerate upper bound rather than a genuine competitor -- the calibrated layer reproduces the strict-registry detections and introduces well-calibrated additional flags on the metadata channel. We assess detector asymmetry with a McNemar paired test and calibration with both a flagged-subset Expected Calibration Error and a strictly proper full-corpus Brier score.

</details>


### [190] [LLM Agents Can See Code Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14061)
*Dongjian Ma,Silin Chen,Yufei Yang,Yulin Shi,Yanfu yan,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究多模态大模型在仓库级代码问题解决中利用可视化仓库表示的效果，发现纯视觉方案效果不佳，但将可视化图谱作为文本的补充模态可使输入token减少26%且保持或提升问题解决准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理几乎完全将代码仓库视为纯文本处理，忽略了人类开发者利用文件夹层次结构、依赖关系等可视化结构来定位大型代码库的认知方式。随着多模态大模型的发展，能否有效利用仓库的可视化表示成为一个开放问题。

Method: 对四种最新多模态模型进行系统实证研究，设计纯视觉、纯文本、混合（文本+视觉图谱）等多种实验条件，在仓库级问题解决任务上评估视觉表示的效果，并分析故障定位、探索深度控制等场景下的表现。

Result: 纯视觉方案会降低准确率并增加token成本，原因是缺乏足够的符号细节导致代理通过重复视觉查询来补偿；而将仓库结构可视化图谱作为文本的补充模态，可减少最高26%的输入token消耗，同时保持或提升问题解决准确率；可视化在故障定位和代理自主控制探索深度时最为有用。

Conclusion: 研究指出了一个实用的混合文本-视觉设计方向，为下一代编码代理提供了借鉴——不应完全抛弃文本而依赖视觉，而是将视觉图谱作为文本的补充，以更高效地理解仓库结构。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models have demonstrated strong performance on software engineering tasks. Yet most agents consume repositories almost entirely as text, which differs from how human developers use visual structure such as folder hierarchies and dependency relationships to orient themselves in large codebases. With multimodal large language models (MLLMs), it is an open question whether agents can effectively benefit from visual representations of repositories. This paper presents the first systematic empirical study of visual repository representations for LLM-based agents on repository-level issue resolution. We evaluate four recent multimodal models. Our results show that a strictly vision-only setup degrades accuracy and increases token cost, because agents lack sufficient symbolic detail and compensate with repeated visual queries. In contrast, integrating visual graphs of repository structure as a supplementary modality alongside standard text interfaces helps agents understand structure more efficiently: input token consumption decreases by up to 26% while issue-resolution accuracy is maintained or improved. Visualization is most useful during fault localization and when the agent autonomously controls exploration depth. These findings point to a practical hybrid text-and-vision design for next-generation coding agents.

</details>


### [191] [FastContext: Training Efficient Repository Explorer for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14066)
*Shaoqiu Zhang,Maoquan Wang,Yuling Shi,Yuhang Wang,Xiaodong Gu,Yongqiang Yao,Rao Fu,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FastContext 是一种专用的探索子代理，将仓库探索与问题求解分离，通过并行工具调用和专业化探索模型（4B-30B参数），在 SWE-bench 等任务中提升最高 5.5% 的解决率并降低最多 60% 的 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 编码智能体在仓库探索环节存在瓶颈：定位相关代码消耗大量 token 预算，无关片段污染上下文，且探索与求解由同一模型完成，导致搜索历史混入求解过程。

Method: 提出 FastContext，一个专用的探索子代理，按需调用，执行并行工具调用，返回精简的文件路径和行范围；使用 4B-30B 参数的专业化探索模型，从强参考模型轨迹引导训练，并通过任务导向的奖励在首次搜索、多轮证据收集和精确引用生成方面进行优化。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Multilingual、SWE-bench Pro 和 SWE-QA 上，集成 FastContext 到 Mini-SWE-Agent 后，端到端解决率最高提升 5.5%，编码智能体 token 消耗最多降低 60%，且额外开销极小。

Conclusion: 仓库探索可以与求解分离，并由专业化模型有效处理。
